      <h3>↺ Dọn duplicate devices</h3>
      <p>Tìm và xoá các entry trùng lặp cùng device ID nhưng khác key (UUID cũ vs fingerprint 64 hex).<br>
      Server ưu tiên giữ key fingerprint (64 hex chars). Chạy 1 lần sau khi deploy để dọn data cũ.</p>
      <div style="display:flex;gap:10px;margin-top:16px;flex-wrap:wrap">
        <button class="btn pri" onclick="runDedup()"><span id="ldr-dedup"></span>▶ Chạy Dedup</button>
        <button class="btn" onclick="loadDevices();goPage('devices',document.getElementById('ni-devices'))">← Về Devices</button>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="tw" id="dedup-result" style="max-width:500px;display:none">
      <div style="padding:12px 16px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--border);font-size:9px;font-family:var(--mono);color:var(--text3);letter-spacing:1.5px">KẾT QUẢ</div>
      <div style="padding:16px 18px" id="dedup-inner"></div>
    </div>
  </div>

</main>
</div>

<script>
'use strict';

const SECRET = 'X9k#mP2$vL8nQ4wR';
let currentApp = 'svrvn';
let apps = [];
let devicesData = [];
let banData = [];
let autoTimer = null;

let currentUrlMap = {};
let allAppSettings = {};

// ── CLOCK ──
function tick(){document.getElementById('sf-clock').textContent=new Date().toLocaleString('vi-VN')}
setInterval(tick,1000);tick();

// ── CRYPTO ──
async function hmac(msg,key){
  const enc=new TextEncoder();
  const k=await crypto.subtle.importKey('raw',enc.encode(key),{name:'HMAC',hash:'SHA-256'},false,['sign']);
  const s=await crypto.subtle.sign('HMAC',k,enc.encode(msg));
  return Array.from(new Uint8Array(s)).map(b=>b.toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('');
}

// ── URL CONFIG ──
function getUrl(){return (document.getElementById('top-url-input').value||'').trim().replace(/\/$/,'')}
function maskUrl(u){try{const p=new URL(u);return p.protocol+'//'+p.hostname+'/••••••••'}catch{return '••••'}}
function applyMasked(u){
  document.getElementById('top-url-input').style.display='none';
  document.getElementById('top-url-masked').style.display='flex';
  document.getElementById('masked-txt').textContent=maskUrl(u);
  document.getElementById('btn-save').style.display='none';
  document.getElementById('btn-clear').style.display='';
}
function showUrlInput(){
  document.getElementById('top-url-masked').style.display='none';
  document.getElementById('top-url-input').style.display='';
  document.getElementById('btn-save').style.display='';
  document.getElementById('btn-clear').style.display='none';
  document.getElementById('top-url-input').focus();
}
function saveUrl(){
  const u=getUrl();
  if(!u){toast('Nhập URL server!','error');return;}
  localStorage.setItem('czAdminUrl',u);
  applyMasked(u);
  toast('Đã lưu & ẩn URL','success');
}
function clearUrl(){
  document.getElementById('top-url-input').value='';
  localStorage.removeItem('czAdminUrl');
  showUrlInput();
  toast('Đã xoá URL','info');
}

// ── AUTO REFRESH ──
function toggleAuto(sec){
  clearInterval(autoTimer);autoTimer=null;
  const dot=document.getElementById('auto-dot'),lbl=document.getElementById('auto-dot-lbl');
  if(+sec>0){dot.classList.add('on');lbl.textContent=sec+'s';autoTimer=setInterval(reloadAll,+sec*1000);}
  else{dot.classList.remove('on');lbl.textContent='OFF';}
}

// ── API ──
async function api(params){
  const url=getUrl();
  if(!url){toast('Nhập URL server!','error');return null;}
  const ts=Math.floor(Date.now()/1000).toString();
  const sig=await hmac(ts,SECRET);
  const qs=new URLSearchParams({...params,ts,sig,app:currentApp});
  try{
    const r=await fetch(url+'?'+qs,{cache:'no-store'});
    const txt=await r.text();
    try{return{ok:r.ok,status:r.status,data:JSON.parse(txt),raw:txt};}
    catch{return{ok:r.ok,status:r.status,data:null,raw:txt};}
  }catch(e){toast('Lỗi kết nối: '+e.message,'error');return null;}
}

// ── NAV ──
function goPage(name,el){
  document.querySelectorAll('.page').forEach(p=>p.classList.remove('act'));
  document.querySelectorAll('.nav-item').forEach(n=>n.classList.remove('act'));
  const pg=document.getElementById('page-'+name);
  if(pg)pg.classList.add('act');
  if(el)el.classList.add('act');
}

// ── APPS ──
async function loadApps(){
  const res=await api({action:'getapps',admin:'mkadmin'});
  if(res&&res.ok&&Array.isArray(res.data)&&res.data.length){apps=res.data;}
  else apps=['svrvn','svrtw','hong','svrtest'];
  if(!apps.includes(currentApp))currentApp=apps[0];
  renderAppSwitcher();
  reloadAll();
}

function renderAppSwitcher(){
  const c=document.getElementById('app-switcher');
  c.innerHTML='';
  const ordered=[...apps].sort((a,b)=>{
    const da=String(a).toLowerCase()==='svrtest';
    const db=String(b).toLowerCase()==='svrtest';
    if(da&&!db)return 1;
    if(!da&&db)return -1;
    return String(a).localeCompare(String(b));
  });
  ordered.forEach(a=>{
    const isDev=String(a).toLowerCase()==='svrtest';
    const b=document.createElement('button');
    b.className='app-btn'+(a===currentApp?' act':'')+(isDev?' dev-app-btn':'');
    b.innerHTML=isDev?'<span class="dev-kicker">DEV</span><span>SVRTEST</span>':'<span>'+a.toUpperCase()+'</span>';
    b.onclick=()=>switchApp(a);
    c.appendChild(b);
  });
}

function switchApp(a){
  if(!apps.includes(a))return;
  currentApp=a;
  const tag=a.toUpperCase();
  ['tag-devices','tag-banlist','tag-banstats','tag-hash','tag-devmode','tag-urlmap','tag-tokenbl','tag-dedup']
    .forEach(id=>{const e=document.getElementById(id);if(e)e.textContent=tag;});
  document.getElementById('sf-app').textContent='APP: '+tag;
  document.getElementById('app-notice').textContent='⬡ '+tag+' — isolated data';
  renderAppSwitcher();
  reloadAll();
}

function reloadAll(){
  loadDevices();loadBanList();loadBanStats();loadHash();loadDevMode();
}

// ── ANIMATE NUM ──
function animN(el,target){
  const start=parseInt(el.textContent)||0;
  if(start===target){el.textContent=target;return;}
  const diff=target-start,dur=500,t0=performance.now();
  function step(now){
    const t=Math.min((now-t0)/dur,1),e=1-Math.pow(1-t,3);
    el.textContent=Math.round(start+diff*e);
    if(t<1)requestAnimationFrame(step);
  }
  requestAnimationFrame(step);
}

// ── DEVICES ──
async function loadDevices(){
  const ldr=document.getElementById('ldr-dev');ldr.innerHTML='<span class="loader"></span> ';
  const res=await api({action:'devicelist',admin:'mkadmin'});
  ldr.innerHTML='';
  if(!res||!res.ok){toast('Không tải được devices','error');return;}
  devicesData=Object.values(res.data||{});
  const total=devicesData.length;
  const banned=devicesData.filter(d=>d.banned).length;
  const today=new Date();today.setHours(0,0,0,0);
  const todayCount=devicesData.filter(d=>d.created_at&&d.created_at*1000>=today.getTime()).length;
  animN(document.getElementById('sv-total'),total);
  animN(document.getElementById('sv-active'),total-banned);
  animN(document.getElementById('sv-banned'),banned);
  animN(document.getElementById('sv-today'),todayCount);
  document.getElementById('nb-total').textContent=total;
  renderDevices(devicesData);
  toast('Đã tải '+total+' thiết bị','success');
}

function fmtTime(ts){
  if(!ts)return'—';
  const d=new Date(ts*1000);
  return String(d.getHours()).padStart(2,'0')+':'+String(d.getMinutes()).padStart(2,'0')+' '+
    String(d.getDate()).padStart(2,'0')+'/'+String(d.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0')+'/'+d.getFullYear();
}

function renderDevices(list){
  document.getElementById('dev-rc').textContent=list.length+' kết quả';
  const tb=document.getElementById('dev-tbody');
  if(!list.length){tb.innerHTML='<tr><td colspan="7"><div class="es"><span class="ei">✕</span>Không có dữ liệu</div></td></tr>';return;}
  tb.innerHTML=list.map((d,i)=>`
    <tr style="animation-delay:${i*.025}s">
      <td><span class="mono" style="font-size:11px;font-weight:700;cursor:pointer;color:var(--blue)" onclick="copyText('${d.short_id||''}','ID 15 số')" title="${d.short_id||''}">${d.short_id||'<span style="color:var(--text3)">—</span>'}</span></td>
      <td><span class="cid" title="${d.id||''}" onclick="copyText('${d.id||''}','Device ID')">${(d.id||'').substring(0,20)}…</span></td>
      <td class="mono" style="font-size:9px;color:var(--text3)">${d.udid?d.udid.substring(0,14)+'…':'—'}</td>
      <td><span class="badge ${d.app==='svrvn'||d.app==='svrtest'?'blue':'yellow'}">${(d.app||'?').toUpperCase()}</span></td>
      <td class="mono" style="color:var(--text2);font-size:10px">${fmtTime(d.created_at)}</td>
      <td><span class="badge ${d.banned?'banned':'active'}">${d.banned?'BANNED':'ACTIVE'}</span>${d.banned?`<span class="badge gray" style="margin-left:3px;font-size:7px">${(d.ban_reason||'').substring(0,12)}</span>`:''}</td>
      <td><div class="ra">
        <button class="rb view" onclick="showDev(${i})">Detail</button>
        ${d.banned
          ?`<button class="rb unban" onclick="doUnban('${d.id}')">Unban</button>`
          :`<button class="rb ban" onclick="doBan('${d.id}')">Ban</button>`}
      </div></td>
    </tr>`).join('');
}

function filterDevices(q){
  const f=document.getElementById('dev-flt').value;
  let list=devicesData;
  if(f==='active')list=list.filter(d=>!d.banned);
  if(f==='banned')list=list.filter(d=>d.banned);
  if(q){const lq=q.toLowerCase();list=list.filter(d=>(d.short_id||'').includes(lq)||(d.id||'').toLowerCase().includes(lq)||(d.udid||'').toLowerCase().includes(lq)||(d.ban_reason||'').toLowerCase().includes(lq)||(d.ban_label||'').toLowerCase().includes(lq));}
  renderDevices(list);
}

// ── BAN LIST ──
async function loadBanList(){
  const ldr=document.getElementById('ldr-ban');ldr.innerHTML='<span class="loader"></span> ';
  const res=await api({action:'listban',admin:'mkadmin'});
  ldr.innerHTML='';
  if(!res||!res.ok){toast('Không tải được ban list','error');return;}
  banData=Object.entries(res.data||{}).map(([id,v])=>({id,...v}));
  document.getElementById('nb-ban').textContent=banData.length;
  renderBan(banData);
  toast('Đã tải '+banData.length+' ban entry','success');
}

function renderBan(list){
  document.getElementById('ban-rc').textContent=list.length+' kết quả';
  const tb=document.getElementById('ban-tbody');
  if(!list.length){tb.innerHTML='<tr><td colspan="6"><div class="es"><span class="ei">✓</span>Không có thiết bị bị ban</div></td></tr>';return;}
  tb.innerHTML=list.map((d,i)=>{
    const isUdid=d.id.startsWith('udid:');
    const cleanId=d.id.replace(/^udid:/,'');
    return`<tr style="animation-delay:${i*.02}s">
      <td><span class="cid" style="font-size:10px;word-break:break-all" title="${d.id}" onclick="copyText('${cleanId}','ID')">${d.id.substring(0,26)}…</span></td>
      <td><span class="badge ${isUdid?'purple':'blue'}">${isUdid?'UDID':'DID'}</span></td>
      <td><span class="badge banned" style="font-size:8px">${d.reason||'unknown'}</span></td>
      <td style="font-size:9px;color:var(--text2);max-width:160px;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap" title="${d.reason_label||''}">${(d.reason_label||'—').substring(0,40)}</td>
      <td class="mono" style="font-size:9px;color:var(--text3)">${d.banned_at_fmt||(d.banned_at?fmtTime(d.banned_at):'—')}</td>
      <td><div class="ra">
        <button class="rb ico" onclick="copyText('${cleanId}','ID')" title="Copy">⎘</button>
        <button class="rb unban" onclick="doUnban('${cleanId}',true)">Unban</button>
      </div></td>
    </tr>`;}).join('');
}

function filterBan(q){
  if(!q){renderBan(banData);return;}
  const lq=q.toLowerCase();
  renderBan(banData.filter(d=>d.id.toLowerCase().includes(lq)||(d.reason||'').toLowerCase().includes(lq)||(d.reason_label||'').toLowerCase().includes(lq)));
}

async function loadBanStats(){

  animN(document.getElementById('bsv-total'),d.total_banned||0);

const BAR_COLORS=['#4a90e2','#f14c60','#f0a830','#23d18b','#a78bfa','#f97316','#22d3ee','#ec4899','#84cc16','#a855f7'];
function renderBarsChart(reasons){
  const c=document.getElementById('stats-bars');
  const entries=Object.entries(reasons).sort((a,b)=>b[1].count-a[1].count);
  if(!entries.length){c.innerHTML='<div class="es"><span class="ei">✓</span>Chưa có dữ liệu</div>';return;}
  const mx=entries[0][1].count;
  c.innerHTML=entries.map(([k,v],i)=>`<div class="bar-row"><div class="bar-lbl" title="${v.label||k}">${v.label||k}</div><div class="bar-track"><div class="bar-fill" id="bf-${i}" style="background:${BAR_COLORS[i%BAR_COLORS.length]}"></div></div><div class="bar-cnt">${v.count}</div></div>`).join('');
  requestAnimationFrame(()=>entries.forEach(([,v],i)=>{const e=document.getElementById('bf-'+i);if(e)e.style.width=Math.round(v.count/mx*100)+'%';}));
}

// ── HASH CONFIG ──
function renderHashList(elId,arr){
  const el=document.getElementById(elId);if(!el)return;
  if(!arr||!arr.length){el.innerHTML='<span style="color:var(--text3)">(chưa có)</span>';return;}
  el.innerHTML=arr.map((h,i)=>`<div style="padding:2px 0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--border);cursor:pointer;word-break:break-all" onclick="copyText('${h}','Hash #${i+1}')" title="${h}"><span style="color:var(--text3)">#${i+1} </span>${h.substring(0,32)}…</div>`).join('');
}
function renderLearnBadge(elId,on){
  const el=document.getElementById(elId);if(!el)return;
  el.textContent=on?'LEARN ON':'LEARN OFF';el.className='badge '+(on?'on':'off');
}

async function loadHash(){
  const ldr=document.getElementById('ldr-hash');ldr.innerHTML='<span class="loader"></span> ';
  const res=await api({action:'hashinfo',admin:'mkadmin'});
  ldr.innerHTML='';
  if(!res||!res.ok){toast('Không tải được hash config','error');return;}
  const d=res.data||{};
  const lm=d.learn_mode;
  const lb=document.getElementById('lm-badge');
  lb.textContent=lm?'ON':'OFF';lb.className='badge '+(lm?'on':'off');
  document.getElementById('lm-updated').textContent=d.updated_at?fmtTime(d.updated_at):'—';
  renderLearnBadge('lrn-text',  lm&&(d.learn_text  !==false));
  renderLearnBadge('lrn-file',  lm&&(d.learn_file  !==false));
  renderLearnBadge('lrn-lc',    lm&&(d.learn_lc    !==false));
  renderLearnBadge('lrn-bundle',lm&&(d.learn_bundle!==false));
  renderLearnBadge('lrn-dylib', lm&&(d.learn_dylib !==false));
  renderHashList('hl-text',  d.text  ||[]);
  renderHashList('hl-file',  d.file  ||[]);
  renderHashList('hl-lc',    d.lc    ||[]);
  renderHashList('hl-bundle',d.bundle||[]);
  const dylibEl=document.getElementById('hl-dylib');
  const h=d.dylib||'';
  if(dylibEl)dylibEl.innerHTML=h?`<div style="cursor:pointer;word-break:break-all" onclick="copyText('${h}','Dylib Hash')" title="${h}">${h.substring(0,32)}…</div>`:'<span style="color:var(--text3)">(chưa có)</span>';
}

}

    else toast('Lỗi reset','error');
  });
}

    else toast('Lỗi reset','error');
  });
}

function applyDevMode(on){

  const lbl=document.getElementById('dm-lbl');
  const nb=document.getElementById('nb-devmode');
  if(circle){circle.className='dt-circle '+(on?'on':'off');}

  if(nb){nb.style.display=on?'':'none';}
}

async function setDevMode(mode){
  confirm2(
    mode?'Bật Dev Mode?':'Tắt Dev Mode?',
    mode?'App sẽ tự xoá keychain mỗi lần nhận ticket thành công.\nBật khi đang dev / test.':'Keychain không bị xoá nữa.\nTắt khi deploy production.',
    async()=>{
      const res=await api({action:'setdevmode',admin:'mkadmin',mode});
      if(res&&res.ok){
        toast(res.raw||'Đã cập nhật',mode?'warn':'success');
        await loadDevMode();
      }else toast('Lỗi cập nhật dev mode','error');
    }
  );
}

// ── APP CONTROL ──
async function apiForApp(appName,params){const old=currentApp;currentApp=appName;const r=await api(params);currentApp=old;return r;}
function escAttr(s){return String(s??'').replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/"/g,'&quot;').replace(/</g,'&lt;').replace(/>/g,'&gt;')}
function showAppCtlSection(name,btn){
  document.querySelectorAll('#appctl-editor .form-section').forEach(e=>e.classList.remove('act'));
  document.querySelectorAll('#appctl-editor .form-tab').forEach(e=>e.classList.remove('act'));
  const sec=document.getElementById('appctl-sec-'+name); if(sec)sec.classList.add('act');
  if(btn)btn.classList.add('act');
}
function renderAppControl(){
  const grid=document.getElementById('appctl-grid'); if(!grid)return;
  let online=0,offline=0,closed=0,noticeOn=0;
  const entries=Object.entries(allAppSettings||{});
  entries.forEach(([a,s])=>{const st=s.stats||{};online+=st.online||0;offline+=st.offline||0;if(s.closed)closed++;if(s.notice_enabled)noticeOn++;});
  const set=(id,v)=>{const e=document.getElementById(id);if(e)e.textContent=v}; set('appctl-online',online); set('appctl-offline',offline); set('appctl-closed',closed); set('appctl-noticeon',noticeOn);
  if(!entries.length){grid.innerHTML='<div class="es"><span class="ei">◌</span>Không có dữ liệu</div>';return;}
  grid.innerHTML=entries.map(([a,s],idx)=>{const st=s.stats||{};const total=st.total||0;const on=st.online||0;const off=st.offline||0;const ver=escAttr(s.app_version||s.latest_version||'1.0');const minv=escAttr(s.min_required_version||'—');const onPct=total?Math.max(8,Math.round(on/total*100)):0;const offPct=total?Math.max(8,Math.round(off/total*100)):0;const totalPct=total?100:0;const upd=(s.version_check_enabled||s.version_enabled);return `<div class="app-card ${s.closed?'closed':'open'} ${editApp===a?'selected':''}" style="--stagger:${Math.min(idx*55,420)}ms" data-app="${escAttr(a)}" onclick="editAppSettings('${a}')">
    ${editApp===a?'<span class="app-selected-tag">ĐANG SỬA</span>':''}
    <div class="app-card-top">
      <div class="app-name-wrap"><div class="app-avatar">${escAttr(a.substring(0,2).toUpperCase())}</div><div><div class="app-title">${escAttr(a.toUpperCase())}</div><div class="app-sub">VERSION ${ver} · MIN ${minv}</div></div></div>
      <span class="app-pill ${s.closed?'closed':'open'}"><span class="led"></span>${s.closed?'ĐÓNG':'ĐANG MỞ'}</span>
    </div>
    <div class="app-status-strip"><span class="${s.closed?'closed':'open'}"></span></div>
    <div class="app-metrics">
      <div class="metric-box"><div class="metric-lbl">Online</div><div class="metric-val green">${on}</div><div class="metric-bar"><span class="green" style="width:${onPct}%"></span></div></div>
      <div class="metric-box"><div class="metric-lbl">Offline</div><div class="metric-val red">${off}</div><div class="metric-bar"><span class="red" style="width:${offPct}%"></span></div></div>
      <div class="metric-box"><div class="metric-lbl">Total</div><div class="metric-val blue">${total}</div><div class="metric-bar"><span class="blue" style="width:${totalPct}%"></span></div></div>
    </div>
    <div class="app-health">
      <span class="health-chip ${s.notice_enabled?'on':'off'}">NOTICE ${s.notice_enabled?'ON':'OFF'}</span>
      <span class="health-chip ${upd?'on':'off'}">UPDATE ${upd?'SOFT':'OFF'}</span>
      <span class="health-chip ${s.force_update_enabled?'warn':'off'}">${s.force_update_enabled?'FORCE':'NO FORCE'}</span>
    </div>
    <div class="app-info-row"><span>Chạm card để sửa nhanh</span><span>${s.closed?'Maintenance':'Live'}</span></div>
    <div class="app-actions"><button class="btn pri" onclick="event.stopPropagation();editAppSettings('${a}')">Sửa cấu hình</button><button class="btn ${s.closed?'success':'danger'}" onclick="event.stopPropagation();quickSetAppClosed('${a}',${s.closed?0:1})">${s.closed?'Mở app':'Đóng app'}</button></div>
  </div>`}).join('');
}
function editAppSettings(appName){
  editApp=appName;
  document.querySelectorAll('.app-card').forEach(c=>c.classList.toggle('selected',c.getAttribute('data-app')===appName));
  const s=allAppSettings[appName]||{}; const ed=document.getElementById('appctl-editor'); if(ed)ed.style.display='grid';
  const put=(id,v)=>{const e=document.getElementById(id);if(e)e.value=v??''}; const nm=document.getElementById('appctl-edit-name'); if(nm)nm.textContent=appName.toUpperCase();
  put('app-token',s.token||'');
  put('app-version',s.app_version||'1.0'); put('version-enabled',s.version_check_enabled?'1':'0'); put('version-title',s.version_title||''); put('version-message',s.version_message||''); put('version-url',s.version_url||''); put('version-download-url',s.version_download_url||''); put('version-icon-url',s.version_icon_url||''); put('version-open-text',s.version_open_text||''); put('version-download-text',s.version_download_text||''); put('version-close-text',s.version_close_text||'');
  put('maint-title',s.maintenance_title||''); put('maint-message',s.maintenance_message||''); put('maint-url',s.maintenance_url||''); put('maint-icon-url',s.maintenance_icon_url||''); put('maint-open-text',s.maintenance_open_text||''); put('maint-close-text',s.maintenance_close_text||'');
  put('notice-enabled',s.notice_enabled?'1':'0'); put('notice-title',s.notice_title||''); put('notice-message',s.notice_message||''); put('notice-url',s.notice_url||''); put('notice-icon-url',s.notice_icon_url||''); put('notice-open-text',s.notice_open_text||''); put('notice-close-text',s.notice_close_text||''); put('notice-poll-sec',s.notice_poll_sec||15); setNoticeTtlInput(s.notice_ttl_sec||s.notice_duration_sec||s.notice_auto_close_sec||0, s.notice_expires_at||0); updateNoticeDurationPreview();
  put('app-version',s.app_version||s.latest_version||'1.0'); put('app-min-version',s.min_required_version||'1.0'); put('app-build',s.app_build||''); put('version-enabled',(s.version_check_enabled||s.version_enabled)?'1':'0'); put('version-force',s.force_update_enabled?'1':'0'); put('version-title',s.version_title||''); put('version-message',s.version_message||''); put('version-url',s.version_url||''); put('version-download-url',s.version_download_url||''); put('version-icon-url',s.version_icon_url||''); put('version-open-text',s.version_open_text||''); put('version-download-text',s.version_download_text||''); put('version-close-text',s.version_close_text||'');
  const nb=document.getElementById('notice-badge'); if(nb){nb.textContent=s.notice_enabled?'BẬT':'TẮT';nb.className='badge '+(s.notice_enabled?'on':'off');}
}
function noticeTtlSeconds(){const e=document.getElementById('notice-duration-sec');const u=document.getElementById('notice-duration-unit');const n=parseFloat((e&&e.value)||'0')||0;const m=parseInt((u&&u.value)||'3600',10)||3600;return Math.max(0,Math.round(n*m));}
function fmtNoticeTtl(sec){sec=Math.max(0,parseInt(sec||0,10)||0);if(sec<=0)return 'KHÔNG HẾT HẠN';if(sec%86400===0)return (sec/86400)+' NGÀY';if(sec%3600===0)return (sec/3600)+' GIỜ';if(sec%60===0)return (sec/60)+' PHÚT';return sec+' GIÂY';}
function fmtNoticeCountdown(sec){sec=Math.max(0,parseInt(sec||0,10)||0);const d=Math.floor(sec/86400);sec%=86400;const h=Math.floor(sec/3600);sec%=3600;const m=Math.floor(sec/60);const x=sec%60;const pad=n=>String(n).padStart(2,'0');return d>0?`${d}d ${pad(h)}:${pad(m)}:${pad(x)}`:`${pad(h)}:${pad(m)}:${pad(x)}`;}
function setNoticeTtlInput(sec,expiresAt){sec=Math.max(0,parseInt(sec||0,10)||0);const e=document.getElementById('notice-duration-sec'),u=document.getElementById('notice-duration-unit');if(!e||!u)return;if(sec>0&&sec%86400===0){u.value='86400';e.value=sec/86400;}else if(sec>0&&sec%3600===0){u.value='3600';e.value=sec/3600;}else if(sec>0&&sec%60===0){u.value='60';e.value=sec/60;}else{u.value='3600';e.value=sec?Math.round(sec/3600*10)/10:0;}e.dataset.expiresAt=String(expiresAt||0);}
function updateNoticeDurationPreview(){const p=document.getElementById('notice-duration-preview');if(!p)return;const sec=noticeTtlSeconds();const exp=parseInt((document.getElementById('notice-duration-sec')||{}).dataset.expiresAt||'0',10)||0;if(sec<=0){p.textContent='THỜI HẠN THÔNG BÁO: KHÔNG HẾT HẠN';p.classList.remove('expired');return;}if(exp>0){const left=exp-Math.floor(Date.now()/1000);if(left>0){p.textContent=`THỜI HẠN THÔNG BÁO: PHÁT TRONG ${fmtNoticeTtl(sec)} · CÒN ${fmtNoticeCountdown(left)}`;p.classList.remove('expired');}else{p.textContent=`THỜI HẠN THÔNG BÁO: PHÁT TRONG ${fmtNoticeTtl(sec)} · ĐÃ HẾT HẠN`;p.classList.add('expired');}}else{p.textContent=`THỜI HẠN THÔNG BÁO: PHÁT TRONG ${fmtNoticeTtl(sec)}`;p.classList.remove('expired');}}
if(window.noticePreviewTimer)clearInterval(window.noticePreviewTimer);window.noticePreviewTimer=setInterval(updateNoticeDurationPreview,1000);
document.addEventListener('input',function(ev){if(ev.target&&ev.target.id==='notice-duration-sec'){ev.target.dataset.expiresAt='0';updateNoticeDurationPreview();}});
document.addEventListener('change',function(ev){if(ev.target&&ev.target.id==='notice-duration-unit'){const e=document.getElementById('notice-duration-sec');if(e)e.dataset.expiresAt='0';updateNoticeDurationPreview();}});
async function loadAllAppSettings(){const ldr=document.getElementById('ldr-appctl');if(ldr)ldr.innerHTML='<span class="loader"></span> ';const res=await api({action:'listappsettings',admin:'mkadmin'});if(ldr)ldr.innerHTML='';if(!res||!res.ok||!res.data){toast('Không tải được App Control','error');return;}allAppSettings=res.data;renderAppControl();if(!editApp&&Object.keys(allAppSettings).length)editAppSettings(Object.keys(allAppSettings)[0]);else if(editApp&&allAppSettings[editApp])editAppSettings(editApp);}
async function quickSetAppClosed(appName,mode){const res=await apiForApp(appName,{action:'setappclosed',admin:'mkadmin',mode,closed:String(mode)});if(res&&res.ok){toast(`${appName.toUpperCase()}: ${mode?'đã đóng':'đã mở'}`,mode?'warn':'success');await loadAllAppSettings();}else toast('Lỗi đổi trạng thái '+appName,'error');}
async function setAppClosedForEdit(mode){if(editApp)await quickSetAppClosed(editApp,mode);}
async function saveAppTokenForEdit(){if(!editApp)return;const token=(document.getElementById('app-token').value||'').trim();if(!token){toast('Token không được trống','warn');return;}const res=await apiForApp(editApp,{action:'setapptoken',admin:'mkadmin',token});if(res&&res.ok){toast('Đã lưu token '+editApp.toUpperCase(),'success');await loadAllAppSettings();}else toast('Lỗi lưu token','error');}
function confirmResetAppTokenForEdit(){if(!editApp)return;confirm2('Reset token '+editApp.toUpperCase()+'?','Token custom sẽ về mặc định.',async()=>{const res=await apiForApp(editApp,{action:'resetapptoken',admin:'mkadmin'});if(res&&res.ok){toast('Đã reset token','success');await loadAllAppSettings();}else toast('Lỗi reset token','error');});}
async function saveMaintenanceForEdit(){if(!editApp)return;const q={action:'setmaintenance',admin:'mkadmin',title:document.getElementById('maint-title').value||'',message:document.getElementById('maint-message').value||'',url:document.getElementById('maint-url').value||'',icon_url:document.getElementById('maint-icon-url').value||'',open_text:document.getElementById('maint-open-text').value||'',close_text:document.getElementById('maint-close-text').value||''};const res=await apiForApp(editApp,q);if(res&&res.ok){toast('Đã lưu bảo trì','success');await loadAllAppSettings();}else toast('Lỗi lưu bảo trì','error');}
async function saveVersionForEdit(){if(!editApp)return;const q={action:'setversion',admin:'mkadmin',version:document.getElementById('app-version').value||'1.0',min_version:document.getElementById('app-min-version').value||'1.0',build:document.getElementById('app-build').value||'',enabled:document.getElementById('version-enabled').value,force:document.getElementById('version-force').value,title:document.getElementById('version-title').value||'',message:document.getElementById('version-message').value||'',url:document.getElementById('version-url').value||'',download_url:document.getElementById('version-download-url').value||'',icon_url:document.getElementById('version-icon-url').value||'',open_text:document.getElementById('version-open-text').value||'',download_text:document.getElementById('version-download-text').value||'',close_text:document.getElementById('version-close-text').value||''};const res=await apiForApp(editApp,q);if(res&&res.ok){toast('Đã lưu version '+editApp.toUpperCase()+': '+q.version,'success');await loadAllAppSettings();}else toast('Lỗi lưu version','error');}
async function saveNoticeForEdit(){if(!editApp)return;const q={action:'setnotice',admin:'mkadmin',enabled:document.getElementById('notice-enabled').value,title:document.getElementById('notice-title').value||'',message:document.getElementById('notice-message').value||'',url:document.getElementById('notice-url').value||'',download_url:'',icon_url:document.getElementById('notice-icon-url').value||'',open_text:document.getElementById('notice-open-text').value||'',download_text:'',close_text:document.getElementById('notice-close-text').value||'',poll_sec:document.getElementById('notice-poll-sec').value||15,ttl_sec:noticeTtlSeconds()};const res=await apiForApp(editApp,q);if(res&&res.ok){toast('Đã gửi/lưu notice '+editApp.toUpperCase(),'success');await loadAllAppSettings();}else toast('Lỗi lưu notice','error');}

// ── URL MAP ──
async function loadUrlMap(){const ldr=document.getElementById('ldr-urlmap');if(ldr)ldr.innerHTML='<span class="loader"></span> ';document.getElementById('um-app-name').textContent=currentApp.toUpperCase();const res=await api({action:'geturlmap',admin:'mkadmin'});if(ldr)ldr.innerHTML='';if(!res||!res.ok||!res.data){toast('Không tải được URL map: '+(res?res.status:'network'),'error');return;}currentUrlMap=res.data.map||res.data||{};renderUrlMap();toast('Đã tải URL map '+currentApp.toUpperCase(),'success');}
function renderUrlMap(){const g=document.getElementById('urlmap-grid');const entries=Object.entries(currentUrlMap||{});const ta=document.getElementById('url-json');if(ta)ta.value=JSON.stringify(currentUrlMap,null,2);if(!entries.length){g.innerHTML='<div class="es"><span class="ei">◌</span>Không có URL map</div>';return;}g.innerHTML=entries.map(([k,v],i)=>{v=String(v??'');return `<div class="url-item"><span class="url-key" onclick="editUrlIdx(${i})">${escAttr(k)}</span><span class="url-val" onclick="editUrlIdx(${i})" title="${escAttr(v)}">${escAttr(v)||'(trống)'}</span><button class="rb view" onclick="copyUrlIdx(${i})">Copy</button><button class="rb unban" onclick="editUrlIdx(${i})">Sửa</button></div>`}).join('');}
function editUrlIdx(i){const e=Object.entries(currentUrlMap||{})[i];if(!e)return;document.getElementById('um-key').value=e[0];document.getElementById('um-val').value=e[1];document.getElementById('um-val').focus();}
function copyUrlIdx(i){const e=Object.entries(currentUrlMap||{})[i];if(e)copyText(e[1],e[0]);}
function addUrlKey(){document.getElementById('um-key').value='';document.getElementById('um-val').value='';document.getElementById('um-key').focus();}
async function saveUrlKey(){const key=document.getElementById('um-key').value.trim();const value=document.getElementById('um-val').value;if(!key){toast('Nhập key cần lưu','warn');return;}const res=await api({action:'seturlmap',admin:'mkadmin',key,value});if(res&&res.ok){toast('Đã lưu '+key,'success');currentUrlMap=(res.data&&res.data.map)||currentUrlMap;renderUrlMap();await loadDevMode();}else toast('Lỗi lưu URL map','error');}
async function deleteUrlKey(){const key=document.getElementById('um-key').value.trim();if(!key){toast('Nhập key cần xoá','warn');return;}confirm2('Xoá key URL map?',`Xoá key: ${key}`,async()=>{const res=await api({action:'seturlmap',admin:'mkadmin',key,delete:'1'});if(res&&res.ok){toast('Đã xoá '+key,'success');currentUrlMap=(res.data&&res.data.map)||{};renderUrlMap();}else toast('Lỗi xoá key','error');});}
function toggleUrlJson(force){const ta=document.getElementById('url-json'),acts=document.getElementById('url-json-actions');const show=force===undefined?ta.style.display==='none':force;ta.style.display=show?'':'none';acts.style.display=show?'flex':'none';if(show)ta.value=JSON.stringify(currentUrlMap,null,2);}
async function saveUrlJson(){let parsed;try{parsed=JSON.parse(document.getElementById('url-json').value);}catch(e){toast('JSON sai: '+e.message,'error');return;}const res=await api({action:'seturlmap',admin:'mkadmin',json:JSON.stringify(parsed)});if(res&&res.ok){toast('Đã lưu toàn bộ URL map','success');currentUrlMap=(res.data&&res.data.map)||parsed;renderUrlMap();await loadDevMode();}else toast('Lỗi lưu JSON','error');}
function confirmResetUrlMap(){confirm2('Reset URL map về mặc định?','Xoá file custom URL map của app hiện tại.',async()=>{const res=await api({action:'reseturlmap',admin:'mkadmin'});if(res&&res.ok){toast('Đã reset URL map','success');currentUrlMap=(res.data&&res.data.map)||{};renderUrlMap();}else toast('Lỗi reset URL map','error');});}

// ── TOKEN BLACKLIST (via ban + token check workaround) ──
// PHP không expose action=listtokenbl trực tiếp, ta sẽ gợi ý xoá bằng device ID
async function loadTokenBl(){
  const ldr=document.getElementById('ldr-tbl');ldr.innerHTML='<span class="loader"></span> ';
  // Fetch ban list and cross-reference — show entries that were tamper-banned
  const res=await api({action:'listban',admin:'mkadmin'});
  ldr.innerHTML='';
  if(!res||!res.ok){toast('Không tải được dữ liệu','error');return;}
  const bl=res.data||{};
  // Token blacklist entries = those banned for tamper reasons
  const tamperReasons=['text_tampered','file_tampered','bundle_tampered'];
  const tblEntries=Object.entries(bl).filter(([,v])=>tamperReasons.includes(v.reason||'')).map(([id,v])=>({id,...v}));
  document.getElementById('nb-tbl').textContent=tblEntries.length;
  document.getElementById('tbl-rc').textContent=tblEntries.length+' token-blocked entries';
  const tb=document.getElementById('tbl-tbody');
  if(!tblEntries.length){tb.innerHTML='<tr><td colspan="4"><div class="es"><span class="ei">✓</span>Không có token bị block</div></td></tr>';return;}
  tb.innerHTML=tblEntries.map((d,i)=>`<tr style="animation-delay:${i*.02}s">
    <td><span class="cid" style="font-size:10px" onclick="copyText('${d.id}','ID')">${d.id.substring(0,28)}…</span></td>
    <td><span class="badge banned" style="font-size:8px">${d.reason||'unknown'}</span></td>
    <td class="mono" style="font-size:9px;color:var(--text3)">${fmtTime(d.banned_at)}</td>
    <td><div class="ra">
      <button class="rb unban" onclick="doUnban('${d.id}',true)">Unban + Xoá</button>
    </div></td>
  </tr>`).join('');
}

async function clearTokenById(){
  const did=document.getElementById('tbl-inp').value.trim();
  if(!did){toast('Nhập Device ID!','warn');return;}
  // Unban xoá cả token blacklist (PHP unban xoá cntFile và blacklist)
  confirm2('Xoá token blacklist?',`Unban và xoá token BL cho:\n${did}`,async()=>{
    const res=await api({action:'unban',admin:'mkadmin',did});
    if(res&&res.ok){toast('Đã xoá token BL: '+did.substring(0,20),'success');document.getElementById('tbl-inp').value='';await loadTokenBl();}
    else toast('Lỗi','error');
  });
}
async function confirmClearAllTokenBl(){
  confirm2('Xoá TẤT CẢ token blacklist?','Toàn bộ danh sách cấm (bao gồm token BL) sẽ bị xoá. Không hoàn tác!',async()=>{
    const res=await api({action:'unban',admin:'mkadmin',did:''});
    if(res&&res.ok){toast('Đã xoá tất cả','success');await loadTokenBl();await loadBanList();}
    else toast('Lỗi','error');
  });
}

// ── DEDUP ──
async function runDedup(){
  const ldr=document.getElementById('ldr-dedup');ldr.innerHTML='<span class="loader"></span> ';
  const res=await api({action:'dedup',admin:'mkadmin'});
  ldr.innerHTML='';
  const wrap=document.getElementById('dedup-result');
  const inner=document.getElementById('dedup-inner');
  wrap.style.display='';
  if(!res||!res.ok){
    inner.innerHTML='<span style="color:var(--red);font-family:var(--mono);font-size:11px">Lỗi khi chạy dedup: '+(res?res.raw:'')+' </span>';
    toast('Lỗi dedup','error');return;
  }
  const d=res.data||{};
  inner.innerHTML=`
    <div class="info-row"><span class="info-key">ĐÃ XOÁ (DUPLICATE)</span><span class="info-val" style="color:var(--red);font-size:13px;font-weight:700">${d.removed||0}</span></div>
    <div class="info-row"><span class="info-key">CÒN LẠI</span><span class="info-val" style="color:var(--green);font-size:13px;font-weight:700">${d.remaining||0}</span></div>
  `;
  toast(`Dedup: xoá ${d.removed||0}, còn ${d.remaining||0}`,'success');
}

// ── BAN / UNBAN ──
async function doBan(did){
  confirm2('Ban thiết bị?',`Bạn có chắc muốn ban:\n${did}`,async()=>{
    const res=await api({action:'ban',admin:'mkadmin',did,reason:'manual'});
    if(res&&res.ok){toast('Đã ban: '+did.substring(0,22),'success');await loadDevices();}
    else toast('Lỗi ban','error');
  });
}
async function doUnban(did,reloadBan=false){
  const res=await api({action:'unban',admin:'mkadmin',did});
  if(res&&res.ok){
    toast('Đã unban: '+did.substring(0,22),'success');
    if(reloadBan){await loadBanList();await loadDevices();}
    else await loadDevices();
  }else toast('Lỗi unban','error');
}
async function quickBan(){
  const did=document.getElementById('qi-id').value.trim();
  const reason=document.getElementById('qi-reason').value;
  if(!did){toast('Nhập Device ID!','warn');return;}
  confirm2('Ban nhanh?',`Ban ID:\n${did}\n\nLý do: ${reason}`,async()=>{
    const res=await api({action:'ban',admin:'mkadmin',did,reason});
    if(res&&res.ok){toast('Đã ban: '+did.substring(0,22),'success');document.getElementById('qi-id').value='';await loadDevices();}
    else toast('Lỗi ban: '+(res?res.raw:'network'),'error');
  });
}
async function quickUnban(){
  const did=document.getElementById('qi-id').value.trim();
  if(!did){toast('Nhập Device ID!','warn');return;}
  confirm2('Unban nhanh?',`Xác nhận unban:\n${did}`,async()=>{
    const res=await api({action:'unban',admin:'mkadmin',did});
    if(res&&res.ok){toast('Đã unban: '+did.substring(0,22),'success');document.getElementById('qi-id').value='';await loadDevices();await loadBanList();}
    else toast('Lỗi unban','error');
  });
}

    if(res&&res.ok){toast('Đã unban: '+did.substring(0,22),'success');document.getElementById('unban-inp').value='';await loadBanList();await loadDevices();}
    else toast('Lỗi unban','error');
  });
}

async function confirmClearDevices(){
  confirm2('Xoá tất cả devices?','Danh sách thiết bị sẽ bị xoá hoàn toàn. Không hoàn tác!',async()=>{
    const res=await api({action:'resetdevices',admin:'mkadmin'});
    if(res&&res.ok){toast('Đã xoá tất cả thiết bị','success');await loadDevices();}
    else toast('Lỗi','error');
  });
}

// ── DEVICE DETAIL ──
function showDev(idx){
  const d=devicesData[idx];if(!d)return;
  const fields=[
    ['ID 15 số',d.short_id||'—'],['Device ID',d.id||'—'],['UDID',d.udid||'—'],
    ['App',(d.app||'?').toUpperCase()],['Đăng ký',fmtTime(d.created_at)],
    ['Trạng thái',d.banned?'⛔ BANNED':'✓ ACTIVE'],['Lý do ban',d.ban_reason||'—'],
    ['Chi tiết ban',d.ban_label||'—'],
  ];
  document.getElementById('dmo-content').innerHTML=fields.map(([k,v])=>`<div class="dr"><span class="dk">${k.toUpperCase()}</span><span class="dv" onclick="copyText('${(v||'').replace(/'/g,'')}}','${k}')">${v}</span></div>`).join('');
  document.getElementById('dmo-actions').innerHTML=d.banned
    ?`<button class="btn success" onclick="doUnban('${d.id}');closeDmo()">✓ Unban</button>`
    :`<button class="btn danger" onclick="doBan('${d.id}');closeDmo()">⛔ Ban</button>`;
  document.getElementById('dmo').classList.add('open');
}
function closeDmo(){document.getElementById('dmo').classList.remove('open');}
document.getElementById('dmo').addEventListener('click',e=>{if(e.target===document.getElementById('dmo'))closeDmo();});

// ── EXPORT CSV ──
function exportCSV(type){
  let rows,headers,filename;
  if(type==='devices'){
    headers=['Device ID','UDID','App','Đăng ký','Trạng thái','Lý do ban'];
    rows=devicesData.map(d=>[d.id,d.udid||'',(d.app||'').toUpperCase(),fmtTime(d.created_at),d.banned?'BANNED':'ACTIVE',d.ban_reason||'']);
    filename='devices_'+currentApp+'_'+Date.now()+'.csv';
  }else{
    headers=['Device ID','Loại','Lý do','Chi tiết','Thời gian ban'];
    rows=banData.map(d=>[d.id,d.id.startsWith('udid:')?'UDID':'DID',d.reason||'',d.reason_label||'',d.banned_at?fmtTime(d.banned_at):'']);
    filename='banlist_'+currentApp+'_'+Date.now()+'.csv';
  }
  const csv=[headers,...rows].map(r=>r.map(v=>`"${String(v).replace(/"/g,'""')}"`).join(',')).join('\n');
  const blob=new Blob(['\uFEFF'+csv],{type:'text/csv;charset=utf-8;'});
  const a=document.createElement('a');a.href=URL.createObjectURL(blob);a.download=filename;a.click();
  toast('Đã xuất: '+filename,'success');
}

// ── COPY ──
function copyText(text,label){
  if(!text||text==='—'||text==='undefined')return;
  navigator.clipboard.writeText(text).then(()=>toast('Đã copy '+label,'info')).catch(()=>{
    const ta=document.createElement('textarea');ta.value=text;document.body.appendChild(ta);ta.select();document.execCommand('copy');document.body.removeChild(ta);
    toast('Đã copy '+label,'info');
  });
}

// ── CONFIRM MODAL ──
let _cmCb=null;
function confirm2(title,msg,cb){
  document.getElementById('cm-title').textContent=title;
  document.getElementById('cm-msg').textContent=msg;
  _cmCb=cb;
  document.getElementById('cmo').classList.add('open');
  document.getElementById('cm-ok').onclick=async()=>{closeCmo();await cb();};
}
function closeCmo(){document.getElementById('cmo').classList.remove('open');_cmCb=null;}
document.getElementById('cmo').addEventListener('click',e=>{if(e.target===document.getElementById('cmo'))closeCmo();});

// ── TOAST ──
let _tt;
function toast(msg,type=''){
  const el=document.getElementById('toast');
  el.textContent=msg;el.className='show '+(type||'');
  clearTimeout(_tt);_tt=setTimeout(()=>{el.className='';},3000);
}



// ── COMMAND CENTER V4 PATCH ──
let currentPageName = 'devices';
const pageLoaders = {
  devices: loadDevices,
  banlist: loadBanList,
  banstats: loadBanStats,
  hash: loadHash,
  devmode: loadDevMode,
  appctl: loadAllAppSettings,
  urlmap: loadUrlMap,
  tokenbl: loadTokenBl
};
function setServerVisual(connected){
  const chip=document.getElementById('server-chip');
  const txt=document.getElementById('server-chip-text');
  if(chip) chip.classList.toggle('connected',!!connected);
  if(txt) txt.textContent=connected?'SERVER ĐÃ KẾT NỐI':'CHƯA KẾT NỐI SERVER';
}
function openServerModal(){
  const mo=document.getElementById('servermo');
  const inp=document.getElementById('server-url-input');
  if(inp) inp.value=localStorage.getItem('czAdminUrl')||document.getElementById('top-url-input').value||'';
  if(mo) mo.classList.add('open');
  setTimeout(()=>inp&&inp.focus(),50);
}
function closeServerModal(){const mo=document.getElementById('servermo'); if(mo)mo.classList.remove('open');}
function getUrl(){return (localStorage.getItem('czAdminUrl')||document.getElementById('top-url-input').value||'').trim().replace(/\/$/,'')}
function applyMasked(u){
  document.getElementById('top-url-input').value=u||'';
  setServerVisual(!!u);
}
function showUrlInput(){openServerModal();}
function saveUrl(){
  const inp=document.getElementById('server-url-input');
  const u=(inp&&inp.value||document.getElementById('top-url-input').value||'').trim().replace(/\/$/,'');
  if(!u){toast('Nhập URL server!','error');return;}
  localStorage.setItem('czAdminUrl',u);
  applyMasked(u);
  closeServerModal();
  toast('Đã lưu kết nối server','success');
  loadApps();
}
function clearUrl(){
  localStorage.removeItem('czAdminUrl');
  document.getElementById('top-url-input').value='';
  const inp=document.getElementById('server-url-input'); if(inp)inp.value='';
  setServerVisual(false);
  toast('Đã xoá kết nối server','info');
}
function reloadCurrentPage(){
  const fn=pageLoaders[currentPageName]||loadDevices;
  fn();
}
function reloadAll(){ reloadCurrentPage(); }
function goPage(name,el){
  currentPageName=name;
  document.querySelectorAll('.page').forEach(p=>p.classList.remove('act'));
  document.querySelectorAll('.nav-item').forEach(n=>n.classList.remove('act'));
  const pg=document.getElementById('page-'+name);
  if(pg)pg.classList.add('act');
  if(el)el.classList.add('act');
  const fn=pageLoaders[name];
  if(fn) setTimeout(fn,40);
  if(name==='dedup'){
    const r=document.getElementById('dedup-result'); if(r)r.style.display='none';
  }
}

// ── INIT ──
window.addEventListener('load',()=>{
  const saved=localStorage.getItem('czAdminUrl');
  if(saved){document.getElementById('top-url-input').value=saved;applyMasked(saved);loadApps();}
  else{setServerVisual(false);openServerModal();}
});


// ── COMMAND CENTER V5 MOTION HELPERS ──
(function(){
  function attachPointerGlow(){
    document.addEventListener('pointermove', function(e){
      const el=e.target.closest && e.target.closest('.btn,.tbtn,.form-tab');
      if(!el) return;
      const r=el.getBoundingClientRect();
      el.style.setProperty('--mx', ((e.clientX-r.left)/Math.max(r.width,1)*100).toFixed(1)+'%');
      el.style.setProperty('--my', ((e.clientY-r.top)/Math.max(r.height,1)*100).toFixed(1)+'%');
    }, {passive:true});
  }
  function staggerCards(root){
    const cards=(root||document).querySelectorAll('.sc,.app-card,.htc,.url-item,tbody tr');
    cards.forEach((el,i)=>{el.style.animationDelay=Math.min(i*24,260)+'ms';});
  }
  window.clazyMotionRefresh=function(){
    staggerCards(document);
  };
  const oldRenderAppControl=window.renderAppControl;
  if(typeof oldRenderAppControl==='function'){
    window.renderAppControl=function(){
      oldRenderAppControl.apply(this,arguments);
      requestAnimationFrame(()=>staggerCards(document.getElementById('page-appctl')||document));
    };
  }
  const oldRenderUrlMap=window.renderUrlMap;
  if(typeof oldRenderUrlMap==='function'){
    window.renderUrlMap=function(){
      oldRenderUrlMap.apply(this,arguments);
      requestAnimationFrame(()=>staggerCards(document.getElementById('page-urlmap')||document));
    };
  }
  const oldGoPage=window.goPage;
  if(typeof oldGoPage==='function'){
    window.goPage=function(name,el){
      const current=document.querySelector('.page.act');
      if(current){current.classList.add('page-leave');setTimeout(()=>current.classList.remove('page-leave'),180);}
      oldGoPage.apply(this,arguments);
      setTimeout(()=>staggerCards(document.getElementById('page-'+name)||document),90);
    };
  }
  window.addEventListener('load',()=>{attachPointerGlow();setTimeout(()=>staggerCards(document),120);});
})();


// ── COMMAND CENTER V6: palette, app filters, live polish ──
(function(){
  const svgSearch='<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2"><circle cx="11" cy="11" r="8"></circle><path d="m21 21-4.35-4.35"></path></svg>';
  const pageMeta={
    devices:['Thiết bị','Danh sách thiết bị, ban nhanh và trạng thái đăng ký.'],
    banlist:['Ban List','Quản lý danh sách chặn và mở chặn thiết bị.'],
    banstats:['Ban Stats','Phân tích lý do vi phạm theo từng app.'],
    hash:['Hash Config','Bật/tắt learn mode và quản lý hash theo từng loại.'],
    devmode:['Dev Mode','Điều khiển dev flag và reset keychain trong giai đoạn test.'],
    appctl:['App Control','Trung tâm điều hành app, version, notice và bảo trì.'],
    urlmap:['URL Map','Sửa link/text động theo từng app, không cần mở PHP.'],
    tokenbl:['Token Blacklist','Xóa token blacklist và trạng thái tamper.'],
    dedup:['Dedup','Dọn dữ liệu thiết bị trùng lặp.']
  };
  function iconSvg(path){return `<svg width="15" height="15" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2">${path}</svg>`}
  function ensureCommandButton(){
    if(document.getElementById('cmd-open-pill')) return;
    const chip=document.getElementById('server-chip');
    if(!chip||!chip.parentElement) return;
    const b=document.createElement('button');
    b.id='cmd-open-pill'; b.className='command-pill'; b.type='button';
    b.innerHTML=`${svgSearch}<span>LỆNH NHANH</span><kbd>⌘K</kbd>`;
    b.onclick=openCommandPalette;
    chip.parentElement.insertBefore(b,chip);
  }
  function ensureCommandPalette(){
    if(document.getElementById('cmd-overlay')) return;
    const ov=document.createElement('div');
    ov.id='cmd-overlay'; ov.className='command-overlay';
    ov.innerHTML=`<div class="command-box"><div class="cmd-search-wrap">${svgSearch}<input id="cmd-search" class="cmd-search" placeholder="Tìm trang, app, thao tác..." autocomplete="off"></div><div id="cmd-list" class="cmd-list"></div></div>`;
    document.body.appendChild(ov);
    ov.addEventListener('click',e=>{if(e.target===ov)closeCommandPalette();});
    const inp=ov.querySelector('#cmd-search');
    inp.addEventListener('input',renderCommandItems);
    inp.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{
      const items=[...document.querySelectorAll('.cmd-item')];
      const cur=document.querySelector('.cmd-item.active');
      let idx=items.indexOf(cur);
      if(e.key==='ArrowDown'){e.preventDefault();idx=Math.min(items.length-1,idx+1);items.forEach(x=>x.classList.remove('active'));if(items[idx])items[idx].classList.add('active');}
      if(e.key==='ArrowUp'){e.preventDefault();idx=Math.max(0,idx-1);items.forEach(x=>x.classList.remove('active'));if(items[idx])items[idx].classList.add('active');}
      if(e.key==='Enter'){e.preventDefault();(document.querySelector('.cmd-item.active')||items[0])?.click();}
      if(e.key==='Escape') closeCommandPalette();
    });
  }
  function commandActions(){
    const base=[
      ['devices','Mở Devices','Xem thiết bị đã đăng ký','D'],['appctl','Mở App Control','Quản lý app / version / notice','A'],['urlmap','Mở URL Map','Sửa link động','U'],['hash','Mở Hash Config','Quản lý hash learn','H'],['devmode','Mở Dev Mode','Bật/tắt dev flag','M'],['banlist','Mở Ban List','Danh sách chặn','B'],['tokenbl','Mở Token Blacklist','Token tamper','T']
    ].map(([page,title,sub,key])=>({type:'page',page,title,sub,key}));
    const appItems=(window.apps||[]).map(a=>({type:'app',app:a,title:'Chọn app '+String(a).toUpperCase(),sub:'Switch workspace app',key:String(a).substring(0,2).toUpperCase()}));
    return [...base,...appItems,{type:'action',title:'Kết nối server',sub:'Mở modal nhập endpoint',key:'S'},{type:'action',title:'Reload trang hiện tại',sub:'Tải lại dữ liệu trang đang mở',key:'R'}];
  }
  function renderCommandItems(){
    const list=document.getElementById('cmd-list'); if(!list) return;
    const q=(document.getElementById('cmd-search')?.value||'').toLowerCase();
    const items=commandActions().filter(x=>(x.title+' '+x.sub+' '+(x.app||'')).toLowerCase().includes(q));
    list.innerHTML=items.map((x,i)=>`<div class="cmd-item ${i===0?'active':''}" data-idx="${i}"><div class="cmd-left"><div class="cmd-icon">${x.type==='app'?String(x.app).substring(0,2).toUpperCase():svgSearch}</div><div><div class="cmd-title">${escAttr(x.title)}</div><div class="cmd-sub">${escAttr(x.sub)}</div></div></div><span class="cmd-key">${escAttr(x.key||'')}</span></div>`).join('')||'<div class="cmd-item"><div class="cmd-left"><div class="cmd-icon">–</div><div><div class="cmd-title">Không có kết quả</div><div class="cmd-sub">Thử từ khóa khác</div></div></div></div>';
    [...list.querySelectorAll('.cmd-item')].forEach((el,i)=>el.onclick=()=>runCommand(items[i]));
  }
  function runCommand(x){
    if(!x) return;
    closeCommandPalette();
    if(x.type==='page'){document.getElementById('ni-'+x.page)?.click();return;}
    if(x.type==='app'){switchApp(x.app);return;}
    if(x.title.includes('Kết nối')){openServerModal();return;}
    if(x.title.includes('Reload')){reloadCurrentPage();return;}
  }
  window.openCommandPalette=function(){ensureCommandPalette();renderCommandItems();const ov=document.getElementById('cmd-overlay');ov.classList.add('open');setTimeout(()=>document.getElementById('cmd-search')?.focus(),30);};
  window.closeCommandPalette=function(){document.getElementById('cmd-overlay')?.classList.remove('open');};
  function enhanceNavIcons(){
    document.querySelectorAll('.nav-item svg').forEach(svg=>{if(svg.parentElement.classList.contains('nav-icon-wrap'))return;const w=document.createElement('span');w.className='nav-icon-wrap';svg.parentNode.insertBefore(w,svg);w.appendChild(svg);});
  }
  function ensurePageHeads(){
    document.querySelectorAll('.page').forEach(pg=>{
      const name=pg.id.replace('page-',''); if(pg.querySelector('.page-command-head')) return;
      const ph=pg.querySelector('.ph'); if(!ph) return;
      const meta=pageMeta[name]||['Trang quản lý','Điều khiển nhanh và dữ liệu realtime.'];
      const head=document.createElement('div');head.className='page-command-head';
      head.innerHTML=`<div><div class="page-kicker">${escAttr(meta[0])}</div><div class="page-desc">${escAttr(meta[1])}</div></div><div class="quick-actions"><button class="quick-chip" type="button" data-act="reload">Reload</button><button class="quick-chip" type="button" data-act="command">Command</button></div>`;
      ph.parentNode.insertBefore(head,ph.nextSibling);
      head.querySelector('[data-act="reload"]').onclick=()=>reloadCurrentPage();
      head.querySelector('[data-act="command"]').onclick=()=>openCommandPalette();
    });
  }
  function ensureAppToolbar(){
    const grid=document.getElementById('appctl-grid'); if(!grid||document.getElementById('app-toolbar')) return;
    const bar=document.createElement('div');bar.id='app-toolbar';bar.className='app-toolbar';
    bar.innerHTML=`<input class="app-search" id="app-search" placeholder="Tìm app theo tên, version, trạng thái..." autocomplete="off"><div class="app-filter-group"><button class="filter-chip act" data-filter="all">ALL</button><button class="filter-chip" data-filter="open">MỞ</button><button class="filter-chip" data-filter="closed">ĐÓNG</button><button class="filter-chip" data-filter="notice">NOTICE</button><button class="filter-chip" data-filter="force">FORCE</button></div>`;
    grid.parentNode.insertBefore(bar,grid);
    bar.querySelector('#app-search').addEventListener('input',applyAppFilter);
    bar.querySelectorAll('.filter-chip').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{bar.querySelectorAll('.filter-chip').forEach(x=>x.classList.remove('act'));b.classList.add('act');applyAppFilter();});
  }
  function decorateAppCards(){
    const grid=document.getElementById('appctl-grid'); if(!grid) return;
    grid.querySelectorAll('.app-card').forEach(card=>{
      if(!card.querySelector('.app-signal')){
        const total=parseInt(card.querySelector('.metric-val.blue')?.textContent||'0')||0;
        const online=parseInt(card.querySelector('.metric-val.green')?.textContent||'0')||0;
        const pct=total?Math.round(online/total*100):0;
        card.style.setProperty('--health',Math.max(12,Math.round(pct/100*360))+'deg');
        const signal=document.createElement('div');signal.className='app-signal';signal.innerHTML=`<span>${pct}%</span>`;card.appendChild(signal);
        const line=document.createElement('div');line.className='app-state-line';line.innerHTML=`<span style="width:${Math.max(pct,card.classList.contains('closed')?100:8)}%"></span>`;card.appendChild(line);
      }
    });
    applyAppFilter();
  }
  function applyAppFilter(){
    const grid=document.getElementById('appctl-grid'); if(!grid) return;
    const q=(document.getElementById('app-search')?.value||'').toLowerCase().trim();
    const f=document.querySelector('#app-toolbar .filter-chip.act')?.dataset.filter||'all';
    let visible=0;
    grid.querySelectorAll('.app-card').forEach(card=>{
      const text=card.textContent.toLowerCase();
      let ok=!q||text.includes(q);
      if(f==='open') ok=ok&&card.classList.contains('open');
      if(f==='closed') ok=ok&&card.classList.contains('closed');
      if(f==='notice') ok=ok&&/notice\s*on/i.test(card.textContent);
      if(f==='force') ok=ok&&/force/i.test(card.textContent)&&!/no force/i.test(card.textContent);
      card.classList.toggle('is-hidden',!ok);
      if(ok) visible++;
      card.classList.toggle('focused-by-search',!!q&&ok);
    });
    grid.classList.toggle('filtered-empty',visible===0);
  }
  function markLoading(fnName){
    const old=window[fnName];
    if(typeof old!=='function'||old.__v6) return;
    const wrapped=async function(){
      const page=document.querySelector('.page.act .tw,.page.act .clean-card,.page.act .app-grid')||document.querySelector('.page.act');
      page?.classList.add('data-loading');
      try{return await old.apply(this,arguments);}finally{setTimeout(()=>page?.classList.remove('data-loading'),260);}
    }; wrapped.__v6=true; window[fnName]=wrapped;
  }
  function attachSpotlight(){
    // Throttle spotlight updates to animation frames to avoid jitter.
    let raf=0;
    let lastX=0, lastY=0;
    const apply=()=>{
      raf=0;
      const x=(lastX/window.innerWidth)*100;
      const y=(lastY/window.innerHeight)*100;
      document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--spot-x',x.toFixed(1)+'%');
      document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--spot-y',y.toFixed(1)+'%');
    };
    document.addEventListener('pointermove',e=>{
      lastX=e.clientX; lastY=e.clientY;
      if(!raf) raf=requestAnimationFrame(apply);
    },{passive:true});
  }
  const oldRenderAppControl=window.renderAppControl;
  if(typeof oldRenderAppControl==='function'){
    window.renderAppControl=function(){oldRenderAppControl.apply(this,arguments);ensureAppToolbar();requestAnimationFrame(decorateAppCards);};
  }
  const oldGoPage=window.goPage;
  if(typeof oldGoPage==='function'){
    window.goPage=function(){oldGoPage.apply(this,arguments);setTimeout(()=>{ensurePageHeads();if(arguments[0]==='appctl'){ensureAppToolbar();decorateAppCards();}},80);};
  }
  document.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if((e.metaKey||e.ctrlKey)&&e.key.toLowerCase()==='k'){e.preventDefault();openCommandPalette();} if(e.key==='Escape')closeCommandPalette();});
  window.addEventListener('load',()=>{
    ensureCommandButton();ensureCommandPalette();enhanceNavIcons();ensurePageHeads();ensureAppToolbar();decorateAppCards();attachSpotlight();
    ['loadDevices','loadBanList','loadBanStats','loadHash','loadDevMode','loadUrlMap','loadTokenBl','loadAllAppSettings'].forEach(markLoading);
  });
})();



// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// COMMAND CENTER V7 STABILITY PATCH
// Rebuilds fragile App Control render, fixes page loading + layout filtering.
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
(function(){
  const loaderMap = {
    devices: () => window.loadDevices && loadDevices(),
    banlist: () => window.loadBanList && loadBanList(),
    banstats: () => window.loadBanStats && loadBanStats(),
    hash: () => window.loadHash && loadHash(),
    devmode: () => window.loadDevMode && loadDevMode(),
    appctl: () => window.loadAllAppSettings && loadAllAppSettings(),
    urlmap: () => window.loadUrlMap && loadUrlMap(),
    tokenbl: () => window.loadTokenBl && loadTokenBl()
  };
  function safeEsc(v){
    try { return escAttr(v); } catch(e) { return String(v??'').replace(/[&<>\"]/g, m=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;'}[m])); }
  }
  function pageName(){ return window.currentPageName || document.querySelector('.page.act')?.id?.replace('page-','') || 'devices'; }
  window.reloadCurrentPage = function(){
    const name = pageName();
    const fn = loaderMap[name] || loaderMap.devices;
    return fn && fn();
  };
  window.reloadAll = window.reloadCurrentPage;
  window.goPage = function(name, el){
    window.currentPageName = name;
    document.querySelectorAll('.page').forEach(p=>p.classList.remove('act','page-leave'));
    document.querySelectorAll('.nav-item').forEach(n=>n.classList.remove('act'));
    const pg=document.getElementById('page-'+name);
    if(pg) pg.classList.add('act');
    if(el) el.classList.add('act');
    if(name==='dedup') document.getElementById('dedup-result')?.style && (document.getElementById('dedup-result').style.display='none');
    const fn=loaderMap[name];
    if(fn) setTimeout(fn, 45);
    setTimeout(()=>{
      if(typeof ensurePageHeads==='function') try{ensurePageHeads();}catch(e){}
      if(name==='appctl') { try{ensureAppToolbarV7(); applyAppFilterV7();}catch(e){} }
    }, 80);
  };
  window.ensureAppToolbarV7 = function(){
    const grid=document.getElementById('appctl-grid');
    if(!grid) return;
    let bar=document.getElementById('app-toolbar');
    if(!bar){
      bar=document.createElement('div');
      bar.id='app-toolbar';
      bar.className='app-toolbar';
      bar.innerHTML='<input class="app-search" id="app-search" placeholder="Tìm app, version, trạng thái..." autocomplete="off"><div class="app-filter-group"><button class="filter-chip act" data-filter="all">ALL</button><button class="filter-chip" data-filter="open">MỞ</button><button class="filter-chip" data-filter="closed">ĐÓNG</button><button class="filter-chip" data-filter="notice">NOTICE</button><button class="filter-chip" data-filter="force">FORCE</button></div>';
      grid.parentNode.insertBefore(bar, grid);
      bar.querySelector('#app-search').addEventListener('input', applyAppFilterV7);
      bar.querySelectorAll('.filter-chip').forEach(btn=>btn.addEventListener('click',()=>{
        bar.querySelectorAll('.filter-chip').forEach(x=>x.classList.remove('act'));
        btn.classList.add('act');
        applyAppFilterV7();
      }));
    }
  };
  window.applyAppFilterV7 = function(){
    const grid=document.getElementById('appctl-grid'); if(!grid) return;
    const q=(document.getElementById('app-search')?.value||'').toLowerCase().trim();
    const f=document.querySelector('#app-toolbar .filter-chip.act')?.dataset.filter || 'all';
    let visible=0;
    grid.querySelectorAll('.app-card').forEach(card=>{
      let ok=!q || card.textContent.toLowerCase().includes(q);
      if(f==='open') ok = ok && card.dataset.closed === '0';
      if(f==='closed') ok = ok && card.dataset.closed === '1';
      if(f==='notice') ok = ok && card.dataset.notice === '1';
      if(f==='force') ok = ok && card.dataset.force === '1';
      card.classList.toggle('is-hidden', !ok);
      card.classList.toggle('focused-by-search', !!q && ok);
      if(ok) visible++;
    });
    grid.classList.toggle('filtered-empty', visible===0);
  };
  window.renderAppControl = function(){
    const grid=document.getElementById('appctl-grid'); if(!grid) return;
    let online=0, offline=0, closed=0, noticeOn=0;
    const entries=Object.entries(window.allAppSettings||{});
    entries.forEach(([a,s])=>{const st=s.stats||{}; online+=st.online||0; offline+=st.offline||0; if(s.closed)closed++; if(s.notice_enabled)noticeOn++;});
    const set=(id,v)=>{const e=document.getElementById(id); if(e)e.textContent=v;};
    set('appctl-online',online); set('appctl-offline',offline); set('appctl-closed',closed); set('appctl-noticeon',noticeOn);
    if(!entries.length){ grid.innerHTML='<div class="es"><span class="ei">•</span>Không có dữ liệu app</div>'; return; }
    grid.innerHTML = entries.map(([a,s],i)=>{
      const st=s.stats||{};
      const total=Number(st.total||0), on=Number(st.online||0), off=Number(st.offline||0);
      const pct=total?Math.round(on/total*100):0;
      const ver=safeEsc(s.app_version||s.latest_version||'1.0');
      const minv=safeEsc(s.min_required_version||'—');
      const isClosed=!!s.closed, notice=!!s.notice_enabled, soft=!!(s.version_check_enabled||s.version_enabled), force=!!s.force_update_enabled;
      return `<div class="app-card ${isClosed?'closed':'open'} ${window.editApp===a?'selected':''}" data-app="${safeEsc(a)}" data-closed="${isClosed?'1':'0'}" data-notice="${notice?'1':'0'}" data-force="${force?'1':'0'}" style="--health:${Math.max(12,Math.round(pct/100*360))}deg;animation-delay:${Math.min(i*34,300)}ms" onclick="editAppSettings('${safeEsc(a)}')">
        <div class="app-card-top">
          <div class="app-name-wrap"><div class="app-avatar">${safeEsc(String(a).substring(0,2).toUpperCase())}</div><div><div class="app-title">${safeEsc(String(a).toUpperCase())}</div><div class="app-sub">VER ${ver} · MIN ${minv}</div></div></div>
          <span class="app-pill ${isClosed?'closed':'open'}"><span class="led"></span>${isClosed?'ĐÓNG':'MỞ'}</span>
        </div>
        <div class="app-signal"><span>${pct}%</span></div>
        <div class="app-metrics">
          <div class="metric-box"><div class="metric-lbl">Online</div><div class="metric-val green">${on}</div></div>
          <div class="metric-box"><div class="metric-lbl">Offline</div><div class="metric-val red">${off}</div></div>
          <div class="metric-box"><div class="metric-lbl">Total</div><div class="metric-val blue">${total}</div></div>
        </div>
        <div class="app-state-line"><span style="width:${Math.max(pct,isClosed?100:8)}%"></span></div>
        <div class="app-info-row"><span>Trạng thái thông báo</span><span class="app-flags"><span class="badge ${notice?'on':'gray'}">NOTICE ${notice?'ON':'OFF'}</span><span class="badge ${soft?'blue':'gray'}">UPDATE ${soft?'SOFT':'OFF'}</span><span class="badge ${force?'banned':'gray'}">${force?'FORCE':'NO FORCE'}</span></span></div>
        <div class="app-actions"><button class="btn pri" onclick="event.stopPropagation();editAppSettings('${safeEsc(a)}')">Sửa cấu hình</button><button class="btn ${isClosed?'success':'danger'}" onclick="event.stopPropagation();quickSetAppClosed('${safeEsc(a)}',${isClosed?0:1})">${isClosed?'Mở app':'Đóng app'}</button></div>
      </div>`;
    }).join('');
    ensureAppToolbarV7();
    applyAppFilterV7();
  };
  const oldEdit = window.editAppSettings;
  if(typeof oldEdit==='function'){
    window.editAppSettings=function(appName){
      oldEdit.apply(this, arguments);
      document.querySelectorAll('.app-card').forEach(c=>c.classList.toggle('selected', c.dataset.app===appName));
    };
  }
  const oldLoad = window.loadAllAppSettings;
  if(typeof oldLoad==='function'){
    window.loadAllAppSettings=async function(){
      const area=document.getElementById('appctl-grid'); area?.classList.add('data-loading');
      try { return await oldLoad.apply(this, arguments); }
      finally { setTimeout(()=>area?.classList.remove('data-loading'),220); }
    };
  }
  window.addEventListener('load',()=>{
    setTimeout(()=>{ensureAppToolbarV7(); applyAppFilterV7();},180);
  });
})();

/* COMMAND CENTER V9 RUNTIME FIXES */
(function(){
  function removeAppToolbar(){
    document.querySelectorAll('#app-toolbar,.app-toolbar').forEach(e=>e.remove());
    const grid=document.getElementById('appctl-grid');
    if(grid){grid.classList.remove('filtered-empty');grid.querySelectorAll('.app-card').forEach(c=>c.classList.remove('is-hidden','focused-by-search'));}
  }
  window.ensureAppToolbarV7=function(){removeAppToolbar();};
  window.ensureAppToolbarV8=function(){removeAppToolbar();};
  window.applyAppFilterV7=function(){removeAppToolbar();};
  window.applyAppFilterV8=function(){removeAppToolbar();};
  const oldRender=window.renderAppControl;
  if(typeof oldRender==='function'){
    window.renderAppControl=function(){
      const out=oldRender.apply(this,arguments);
      removeAppToolbar();
      requestAnimationFrame(removeAppToolbar);
      return out;
    };
  }
  const oldGo=window.goPage;
  if(typeof oldGo==='function'){
    window.goPage=function(name,el){
      const out=oldGo.apply(this,arguments);
      if(name==='appctl') setTimeout(removeAppToolbar,80);
      return out;
    };
  }
  window.addEventListener('load',()=>{
    removeAppToolbar();
    setTimeout(removeAppToolbar,160);
    setTimeout(removeAppToolbar,420);
  });
})();

  #appctl-grid .metric-box{border-radius:16px!important;padding:13px 12px!important;min-height:82px!important;background:linear-gradient(180deg,rgba(255,255,255,.045),rgba(255,255,255,.016))!important;border:1px solid rgba(148,163,184,.10)!important}

  #appctl-grid .metric-bar span{height:100%!important;display:block!important;border-radius:inherit!important;transform-origin:left!important}
  #appctl-grid .app-state-line{height:7px!important;margin:0!important;border-radius:999px!important;background:rgba(51,65,85,.50)!important;overflow:hidden!important}
  #appctl-grid .app-state-line span{display:block!important;height:100%!important;border-radius:inherit!important;background:linear-gradient(90deg,#22d3ee,#60a5fa)!important;transition:width .45s cubic-bezier(.2,.8,.2,1)!important}
  #appctl-grid .app-card.closed .app-state-line span{background:linear-gradient(90deg,#fb7185,#ef4444)!important}

  #appctl-grid .app-flags{display:flex!important;gap:6px!important;flex-wrap:wrap!important;justify-content:flex-end!important;max-width:none!important}
  #appctl-grid .app-actions{display:grid!important;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr!important;gap:10px!important;margin-top:auto!important}
  #appctl-grid .app-actions .btn{height:42px!important;border-radius:15px!important;min-width:0!important;font-size:12px!important;font-weight:800!important;white-space:nowrap!important;justify-content:center!important}
  #appctl-grid .selected-badge{position:absolute;top:12px;right:12px;height:24px;padding:0 9px;border-radius:999px;background:rgba(96,165,250,.14);border:1px solid rgba(96,165,250,.30);color:#93c5fd;font-family:var(--mono);font-size:7px;font-weight:900;letter-spacing:1px;display:none;align-items:center;z-index:3}
  #appctl-grid .app-card.selected .selected-badge{display:flex!important}
  @media(max-width:720px){#appctl-grid{grid-template-columns:1fr!important}.app-card.dev-card{grid-column:auto!important}#appctl-grid .app-metrics{grid-template-columns:1fr!important}#appctl-grid .app-actions{grid-template-columns:1fr!important}}
  `;
  const style = document.createElement('style');
  style.id = 'app-control-v10-clean-style';
  style.textContent = css;
  document.head.appendChild(style);

  function esc(v){return String(v??'').replace(/[&<>"']/g,m=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[m]));}
  function jesc(v){return String(v??'').replace(/\\/g,'\\\\').replace(/'/g,"\\'").replace(/\n/g,' ');}
  function pct(n,d){return d?Math.round(Number(n||0)/Number(d||0)*100):0;}
  function isDevApp(name){return String(name||'').toLowerCase()==='svrtest';}
  function removeToolbar(){document.querySelectorAll('#app-toolbar,.app-toolbar').forEach(e=>e.remove());}

  window.renderAppControl = function(){
    const grid=document.getElementById('appctl-grid'); if(!grid) return;
    removeToolbar();
    const data=(typeof allAppSettings!=='undefined'&&allAppSettings)?allAppSettings:(window.allAppSettings||{});
    const entries=Object.entries(data).sort(([a],[b])=>{
      if(isDevApp(a) && !isDevApp(b)) return -1;
      if(!isDevApp(a) && isDevApp(b)) return 1;
      return String(a).localeCompare(String(b));
    });
    let online=0, offline=0, closed=0, noticeOn=0;
    entries.forEach(([,s])=>{const st=s.stats||{}; online+=Number(st.online||0); offline+=Number(st.offline||0); if(s.closed)closed++; if(s.notice_enabled)noticeOn++;});
    const set=(id,v)=>{const e=document.getElementById(id); if(e)e.textContent=v;};
    set('appctl-online',online); set('appctl-offline',offline); set('appctl-closed',closed); set('appctl-noticeon',noticeOn);
    if(!entries.length){grid.innerHTML='<div class="es"><span class="ei">•</span>Không có dữ liệu app</div>';return;}
    grid.innerHTML=entries.map(([a,s],i)=>{
      const st=s.stats||{};
      const total=Number(st.total||0), on=Number(st.online||0), off=Number(st.offline||0);
      const onp=pct(on,total), offp=pct(off,total);
      const isClosed=!!s.closed, notice=!!s.notice_enabled, soft=!!(s.version_check_enabled||s.version_enabled), force=!!s.force_update_enabled;
      const ver=esc(s.app_version||s.latest_version||'1.0'), minv=esc(s.min_required_version||'—');
      const app=esc(a), appjs=jesc(a), dev=isDevApp(a);
      return `<div class="app-card ${isClosed?'closed':'open'} ${dev?'dev-card':''} ${(typeof editApp!=='undefined'&&editApp===a)?'selected':''}" data-app="${app}" data-closed="${isClosed?'1':'0'}" data-notice="${notice?'1':'0'}" data-force="${force?'1':'0'}" style="animation-delay:${Math.min(i*38,300)}ms" onclick="editAppSettings('${appjs}')">
        <div class="app-card-top">
          <div class="app-name-wrap"><div class="app-avatar">${esc(String(a).slice(0,2).toUpperCase())}</div><div><div class="app-title">${esc(String(a).toUpperCase())}</div><div class="app-sub">VER ${ver} · MIN ${minv}</div>${dev?'<div class="app-dev-mark"><span class="dev-dot"></span>DEV SERVER</div>':''}</div></div>
          <span class="app-pill ${isClosed?'closed':'open'}"><span class="led"></span>${isClosed?'ĐÓNG':'MỞ'}</span>
        </div>
        <div class="app-metrics">
          <div class="metric-box"><div class="metric-lbl">Online</div><div class="metric-val green">${on}</div><div class="metric-bar"><span class="green" style="width:${Math.max(onp,on?8:0)}%"></span></div></div>
          <div class="metric-box"><div class="metric-lbl">Offline</div><div class="metric-val red">${off}</div><div class="metric-bar"><span class="red" style="width:${Math.max(offp,off?8:0)}%"></span></div></div>
          <div class="metric-box"><div class="metric-lbl">Total</div><div class="metric-val blue">${total}</div><div class="metric-bar"><span class="blue" style="width:${total?100:0}%"></span></div></div>
        </div>
        <div class="app-state-line"><span style="width:${isClosed?100:Math.max(onp, total?8:0)}%"></span></div>
        <div class="app-info-row"><span>Trạng thái</span><span class="app-flags"><span class="badge ${notice?'on':'gray'}">NOTICE ${notice?'ON':'OFF'}</span><span class="badge ${soft?'blue':'gray'}">UPDATE ${soft?'SOFT':'OFF'}</span><span class="badge ${force?'banned':'gray'}">${force?'FORCE':'NO FORCE'}</span></span></div>
        <div class="app-actions"><button class="btn pri" onclick="event.stopPropagation();editAppSettings('${appjs}')">Sửa cấu hình</button><button class="btn ${isClosed?'success':'danger'}" onclick="event.stopPropagation();quickSetAppClosed('${appjs}',${isClosed?0:1})">${isClosed?'Mở app':'Đóng app'}</button></div>
      </div>`;
    }).join('');
    removeToolbar();
  };

  const oldLoad = window.loadAllAppSettings;
  window.loadAllAppSettings = async function(){
    const ldr=document.getElementById('ldr-appctl'); if(ldr)ldr.innerHTML='<span class="loader"></span> ';
    const area=document.getElementById('appctl-grid'); area?.classList.add('data-loading');
    try{
      const res=await api({action:'listappsettings',admin:'mkadmin'});
      if(!res||!res.ok||!res.data){toast('Không tải được App Control','error');return;}
      if(typeof allAppSettings!=='undefined') allAppSettings=res.data;
      window.allAppSettings=res.data;
      renderAppControl();
      const keys=Object.keys(res.data);
      if((!editApp||!res.data[editApp])&&keys.length) editAppSettings(keys.includes('svrtest')?'svrtest':keys[0]);
      else if(editApp) editAppSettings(editApp);
    } finally {
      if(ldr)ldr.innerHTML=''; area?.classList.remove('data-loading'); removeToolbar();
    }
  };
  const oldEdit=window.editAppSettings;
  if(typeof oldEdit==='function'){
    window.editAppSettings=function(appName){
      oldEdit.apply(this,arguments);
      document.querySelectorAll('#appctl-grid .app-card').forEach(c=>c.classList.toggle('selected',c.dataset.app===appName));
      removeToolbar();
    };
  }
  window.ensureAppToolbarV7=removeToolbar; window.ensureAppToolbarV8=removeToolbar;
  window.applyAppFilterV7=removeToolbar; window.applyAppFilterV8=removeToolbar;
  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',()=>setTimeout(removeToolbar,80));
})();
