  #appctl-grid .app-card.dev-card .app-title::after{content:'DEV SERVER';margin-left:10px;vertical-align:middle;font-family:var(--mono);font-size:8px;font-weight:900;letter-spacing:1.1px;color:#67e8f9;background:rgba(34,211,238,.10);border:1px solid rgba(34,211,238,.30);border-radius:999px;padding:3px 9px;text-shadow:none;white-space:nowrap}
  #appctl-grid .app-card.dev-card .app-sub::before{content:'TEST / DEV · ';color:#67e8f9;font-weight:900;letter-spacing:.8px}
  #appctl-grid .app-card.dev-card .app-state-line span{background:linear-gradient(90deg,#22d3ee,#60a5fa,#a78bfa)!important;box-shadow:0 0 18px rgba(34,211,238,.22)!important}
  #appctl-grid .app-card.dev-card .app-avatar{background:linear-gradient(145deg,rgba(34,211,238,.38),rgba(96,165,250,.20))!important;color:#a5f3fc!important}
  #appctl-grid .app-card.dev-card.selected{box-shadow:0 0 0 2px rgba(34,211,238,.40),0 0 0 7px rgba(34,211,238,.06),0 36px 100px rgba(34,211,238,.20)!important}

  /* Sidebar dev app button */
  .app-switch{display:grid!important;grid-template-columns:repeat(2,minmax(0,1fr))!important;gap:10px!important}
  .app-btn.dev-app-btn{grid-column:1/-1!important;min-height:54px!important;border-color:rgba(34,211,238,.42)!important;background:linear-gradient(135deg,rgba(8,47,73,.74),rgba(15,23,42,.72))!important;color:#67e8f9!important;box-shadow:0 0 0 1px rgba(34,211,238,.08),0 16px 38px rgba(34,211,238,.10)!important}
  .app-btn.dev-app-btn.act{border-color:rgba(34,211,238,.82)!important;background:linear-gradient(135deg,rgba(14,116,144,.42),rgba(37,99,235,.18))!important;box-shadow:0 0 0 1px rgba(34,211,238,.26),0 18px 42px rgba(34,211,238,.18)!important}
  .app-btn.dev-app-btn .dev-kicker{font-size:7px;font-weight:900;letter-spacing:1.4px;border:1px solid rgba(34,211,238,.36);border-radius:999px;padding:3px 7px;background:rgba(34,211,238,.11);color:#67e8f9}

  /* DEV selector button in BASIC */
  #set-dev-app-btn{border-color:rgba(34,211,238,.34)!important;background:rgba(34,211,238,.08)!important;color:#67e8f9!important}
  #set-dev-app-btn.is-current{background:linear-gradient(135deg,rgba(34,211,238,.24),rgba(96,165,250,.12))!important;box-shadow:0 12px 34px rgba(34,211,238,.13)!important}
  @media(max-width:760px){#appctl-grid .app-card.dev-card{grid-column:auto!important}}
  `;
  document.getElementById('app-control-v13-final-style')?.remove();
  const st=document.createElement('style');
  st.id='app-control-v13-final-style'; st.textContent=css; document.head.appendChild(st);

  function getDevAppId(){
    const saved=localStorage.getItem('czDevAppId')||'';
    if(saved && (!Array.isArray(apps) || apps.includes(saved))) return saved;
    const list=Array.isArray(apps)?apps:[];
    return list.find(a=>String(a).toLowerCase()==='svrtest') || list.find(a=>/dev|test/i.test(String(a))) || '';
  }
  function setDevAppId(appName){
    if(!appName) return;
    localStorage.setItem('czDevAppId',appName);
    renderAppSwitcher();
    renderAppControl();
    if(editApp) editAppSettings(editApp);
    toast(appName.toUpperCase()+' đã được đặt làm app DEV','success');
  }
  window.getDevAppId = getDevAppId;
  window.setDevAppId = setDevAppId;

  window.renderAppSwitcher=function(){
    const c=document.getElementById('app-switcher'); if(!c)return;
    const dev=getDevAppId();
    const list=[...(apps||[])].sort((a,b)=>(a===dev?-1:b===dev?1:0));
    c.innerHTML='';
    list.forEach(a=>{
      const isDev=a===dev;
      const b=document.createElement('button');
      b.className='app-btn'+(a===currentApp?' act':'')+(isDev?' dev-app-btn':'');
      b.innerHTML=isDev?'<span class="dev-kicker">DEV</span><span>'+escAttr(a.toUpperCase())+'</span>':'<span>'+escAttr(a.toUpperCase())+'</span>';
      b.onclick=()=>switchApp(a);
      c.appendChild(b);
    });
  };

  window.renderAppControl=function(){
    const grid=document.getElementById('appctl-grid'); if(!grid)return;
    const data=(typeof allAppSettings!=='undefined' && allAppSettings) || window.allAppSettings || {};
    const entries=Object.entries(data);
    let online=0,offline=0,closed=0,noticeOn=0;
    entries.forEach(([a,s])=>{const st=s.stats||{};online+=st.online||0;offline+=st.offline||0;if(s.closed)closed++;if(s.notice_enabled)noticeOn++;});
    const set=(id,v)=>{const e=document.getElementById(id);if(e)e.textContent=v};
    set('appctl-online',online); set('appctl-offline',offline); set('appctl-closed',closed); set('appctl-noticeon',noticeOn);
    if(!entries.length){grid.innerHTML='<div class="es"><span class="ei">◌</span>Không có dữ liệu</div>';return;}
    const dev=getDevAppId();
    const sorted=entries.sort(([a],[b])=>(a===dev?-1:b===dev?1:0));
    grid.innerHTML=sorted.map(([a,s],i)=>{
      const st=s.stats||{}; const total=st.total||0; const on=st.online||0; const off=st.offline||0;
      const onp=total?Math.round(on/total*100):0; const offp=total?Math.round(off/total*100):0;
      const isClosed=!!s.closed, notice=!!s.notice_enabled, soft=!!(s.version_check_enabled||s.version_enabled), force=!!s.force_update_enabled, isDev=a===dev;
      const ver=escAttr(s.app_version||s.latest_version||'1.0'), minv=escAttr(s.min_required_version||'1.0');
      const safe=escAttr(a), appjs=String(a).replace(/\\/g,'\\\\').replace(/'/g,"\\'");
      return `<div class="app-card ${isClosed?'closed':'open'} ${isDev?'dev-card':''} ${editApp===a?'selected':''}" data-app="${safe}" data-closed="${isClosed?'1':'0'}" data-notice="${notice?'1':'0'}" data-force="${force?'1':'0'}" style="animation-delay:${Math.min(i*34,300)}ms" onclick="editAppSettings('${appjs}')">
        <div class="app-card-top">
          <div class="app-name-wrap"><div class="app-avatar">${safe.substring(0,2).toUpperCase()}</div><div><div class="app-title">${safe.toUpperCase()}</div><div class="app-sub">VER ${ver} · MIN ${minv}</div></div></div>
          <span class="app-pill ${isClosed?'closed':'open'}"><span class="led"></span>${isClosed?'ĐÓNG':'MỞ'}</span>
        </div>
        <div class="app-metrics">
          <div class="metric-box"><div class="metric-lbl">Online</div><div class="metric-val green">${on}</div><div class="metric-bar"><span class="green" style="width:${Math.max(onp,on?8:0)}%"></span></div></div>
          <div class="metric-box"><div class="metric-lbl">Offline</div><div class="metric-val red">${off}</div><div class="metric-bar"><span class="red" style="width:${Math.max(offp,off?8:0)}%"></span></div></div>
          <div class="metric-box"><div class="metric-lbl">Total</div><div class="metric-val blue">${total}</div><div class="metric-bar"><span class="blue" style="width:${total?100:0}%"></span></div></div>
        </div>
        <div class="app-state-line"><span style="width:${isClosed?100:Math.max(onp,total?8:0)}%"></span></div>
        <div class="app-info-row"><span>Trạng thái</span><span class="app-flags"><span class="badge ${notice?'on':'gray'}">NOTICE ${notice?'ON':'OFF'}</span><span class="badge ${soft?'blue':'gray'}">UPDATE ${soft?'SOFT':'OFF'}</span><span class="badge ${force?'banned':'gray'}">${force?'FORCE':'NO FORCE'}</span>${isDev?'<span class="badge cyan">DEV</span>':''}</span></div>
        <div class="app-actions"><button class="btn ${isClosed?'success':'danger'}" onclick="event.stopPropagation();quickSetAppClosed('${appjs}',${isClosed?0:1})">${isClosed?'Mở app':'Đóng app'}</button></div>
      </div>`;
    }).join('');
  };

  const oldEdit = window.editAppSettings;
  if(typeof oldEdit==='function'){
    window.editAppSettings=function(appName){
      oldEdit.apply(this,arguments);
      document.querySelectorAll('#appctl-grid .app-card').forEach(c=>c.classList.toggle('selected',c.dataset.app===appName));
      ensureDevButton();
    };
  }
  function ensureDevButton(){
    const basic=document.querySelector('#appctl-sec-basic .form-actions'); if(!basic || !editApp) return;
    let btn=document.getElementById('set-dev-app-btn');
    if(!btn){
      btn=document.createElement('button'); btn.type='button'; btn.id='set-dev-app-btn'; btn.className='btn action-btn';
      btn.onclick=()=>setDevAppId(editApp);
      basic.appendChild(btn);
    }
    const isCurrent=editApp===getDevAppId();
    btn.classList.toggle('is-current',isCurrent);
    btn.textContent=isCurrent?'Đang là app DEV':'Đặt làm app DEV';
  }
  const oldLoad=window.loadAllAppSettings;
  window.loadAllAppSettings=async function(){
    const res=oldLoad?await oldLoad.apply(this,arguments):undefined;
    renderAppSwitcher(); renderAppControl(); if(editApp) editAppSettings(editApp);
    return res;
  };
  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',()=>setTimeout(()=>{renderAppSwitcher(); if(document.getElementById('appctl-grid')) renderAppControl();},120));
})();


/* COMMAND CENTER V14 APP DISPLAY NAMES */
(function(){
  const css = `
  .rename-row{display:grid;grid-template-columns:minmax(0,1fr) 120px;gap:10px;align-items:end;margin-bottom:10px}
  .readonly-chip{height:36px;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;border:1px solid var(--border2);border-radius:12px;background:rgba(15,23,42,.55);font-family:var(--mono);font-size:10px;color:var(--text2);letter-spacing:.8px;white-space:nowrap;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;padding:0 10px}
  #app-display-name{letter-spacing:.3px;font-weight:700}
  #save-app-display-name{border-color:rgba(34,211,238,.34)!important;background:rgba(34,211,238,.08)!important;color:#67e8f9!important}
  #reset-app-display-name{border-color:rgba(148,163,184,.20)!important;background:rgba(15,23,42,.55)!important;color:var(--text2)!important}
  #appctl-grid .app-raw-id{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:8px;letter-spacing:1px;color:rgba(148,163,184,.45);margin-top:4px;text-transform:uppercase;white-space:nowrap;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;max-width:210px}
  .app-btn .app-raw-mini{font-size:7px;letter-spacing:1px;color:rgba(148,163,184,.48);margin-left:4px}
  .app-btn.dev-app-btn .app-raw-mini{color:rgba(103,232,249,.58)}
  #appctl-grid .app-card.dev-card .app-raw-id{color:rgba(103,232,249,.58)}
  `;
  document.getElementById('app-display-name-v14-style')?.remove();
  const style=document.createElement('style'); style.id='app-display-name-v14-style'; style.textContent=css; document.head.appendChild(style);

  function safeText(v){return String(v ?? '');}
  function esc(s){return safeText(s).replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;').replace(/>/g,'&gt;').replace(/"/g,'&quot;');}
  function js(s){return safeText(s).replace(/\\/g,'\\\\').replace(/'/g,"\\'");}
  function loadNameMap(){
    try{const raw=localStorage.getItem('czAppDisplayNames'); const obj=raw?JSON.parse(raw):{}; return obj && typeof obj==='object' ? obj : {};}
    catch{return {};}
  }
  function saveNameMap(map){localStorage.setItem('czAppDisplayNames', JSON.stringify(map||{}));}
  function cleanName(name){return safeText(name).trim().replace(/\s+/g,' ').slice(0,32);}
  function displayName(app){const m=loadNameMap(); return cleanName(m[app]) || safeText(app).toUpperCase();}
  function initials(name, app){const src=cleanName(name)||safeText(app); const parts=src.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean); if(parts.length>=2)return (parts[0][0]+parts[1][0]).toUpperCase(); return src.slice(0,2).toUpperCase();}
  window.getAppDisplayNames = loadNameMap;
  window.getAppDisplayName = displayName;
  window.setAppDisplayName = function(appName, newName){
    const name=cleanName(newName);
    const map=loadNameMap();
    if(name && name.toLowerCase()!==safeText(appName).toLowerCase()) map[appName]=name;
    else delete map[appName];
    saveNameMap(map);
    renderAppSwitcher();
    renderAppControl();
    if(editApp) editAppSettings(editApp);
    toast(name ? ('Đã đổi tên hiển thị: '+name) : ('Đã reset tên '+safeText(appName).toUpperCase()), 'success');
  };

  function ensureRenameFields(){
    const sec=document.querySelector('#appctl-sec-basic .form-grid');
    if(!sec || document.getElementById('app-display-name')) return;
    const wrap=document.createElement('div');
    wrap.className='form-field full';
    wrap.id='app-display-name-wrap';
    wrap.innerHTML=`<label>Tên hiển thị trong panel</label><div class="rename-row"><input class="inp" id="app-display-name" type="text" placeholder="VD: Server Việt Nam"><div class="readonly-chip" id="app-raw-id-chip">APP ID</div></div><div class="hint-line">Chỉ đổi tên hiển thị trên panel, không đổi App ID/server key để tránh mất dữ liệu.</div>`;
    sec.insertBefore(wrap, sec.firstChild);
    const actions=document.querySelector('#appctl-sec-basic .form-actions');
    if(actions && !document.getElementById('save-app-display-name')){
      const save=document.createElement('button'); save.type='button'; save.id='save-app-display-name'; save.className='btn action-btn'; save.textContent='Lưu tên'; save.onclick=()=>{ if(editApp) window.setAppDisplayName(editApp, document.getElementById('app-display-name').value); };
      const reset=document.createElement('button'); reset.type='button'; reset.id='reset-app-display-name'; reset.className='btn action-btn'; reset.textContent='Reset tên'; reset.onclick=()=>{ if(editApp) window.setAppDisplayName(editApp, ''); };
      actions.insertBefore(reset, actions.firstChild);
      actions.insertBefore(save, actions.firstChild);
    }
  }

  const prevRenderSwitcher = window.renderAppSwitcher;
  window.renderAppSwitcher=function(){
    const c=document.getElementById('app-switcher');
    if(!c){ if(prevRenderSwitcher) return prevRenderSwitcher.apply(this,arguments); return; }
    const dev=(typeof getDevAppId==='function')?getDevAppId():'';
    const list=[...(apps||[])].sort((a,b)=>(a===dev?-1:b===dev?1:0));
    c.innerHTML='';
    list.forEach(a=>{
      const isDev=a===dev;
      const label=displayName(a);
      const showRaw=label.toLowerCase()!==safeText(a).toLowerCase();
      const b=document.createElement('button');
      b.className='app-btn'+(a===currentApp?' act':'')+(isDev?' dev-app-btn':'');
      b.innerHTML=isDev
        ? '<span class="dev-kicker">DEV</span><span>'+esc(label)+'</span>'+(showRaw?'<span class="app-raw-mini">'+esc(a.toUpperCase())+'</span>':'')
        : '<span>'+esc(label)+'</span>'+(showRaw?'<span class="app-raw-mini">'+esc(a.toUpperCase())+'</span>':'');
      b.onclick=()=>switchApp(a);
      c.appendChild(b);
    });
  };

  window.renderAppControl=function(){
    const grid=document.getElementById('appctl-grid'); if(!grid)return;
    const data=(typeof allAppSettings!=='undefined' && allAppSettings) || window.allAppSettings || {};
    const entries=Object.entries(data);
    let online=0,offline=0,closed=0,noticeOn=0;
    entries.forEach(([a,s])=>{const st=s.stats||{};online+=Number(st.online||0);offline+=Number(st.offline||0);if(s.closed)closed++;if(s.notice_enabled)noticeOn++;});
    const set=(id,v)=>{const e=document.getElementById(id);if(e)e.textContent=v};
    set('appctl-online',online); set('appctl-offline',offline); set('appctl-closed',closed); set('appctl-noticeon',noticeOn);
    if(!entries.length){grid.innerHTML='<div class="es"><span class="ei">◌</span>Không có dữ liệu</div>';return;}
    const dev=(typeof getDevAppId==='function')?getDevAppId():'';
    const sorted=entries.sort(([a],[b])=>(a===dev?-1:b===dev?1:displayName(a).localeCompare(displayName(b))));
    grid.innerHTML=sorted.map(([a,s],i)=>{
      const st=s.stats||{}; const total=Number(st.total||0); const on=Number(st.online||0); const off=Number(st.offline||0);
      const onp=total?Math.round(on/total*100):0; const offp=total?Math.round(off/total*100):0;
      const isClosed=!!s.closed, notice=!!s.notice_enabled, soft=!!(s.version_check_enabled||s.version_enabled), force=!!s.force_update_enabled, isDev=a===dev;
      const ver=esc(s.app_version||s.latest_version||'1.0'), minv=esc(s.min_required_version||'1.0');
      const name=displayName(a), app=esc(a), appjs=js(a), nameEsc=esc(name), rawDiff=name.toLowerCase()!==safeText(a).toLowerCase();
      return `<div class="app-card ${isClosed?'closed':'open'} ${isDev?'dev-card':''} ${editApp===a?'selected':''}" data-app="${app}" data-closed="${isClosed?'1':'0'}" data-notice="${notice?'1':'0'}" data-force="${force?'1':'0'}" style="animation-delay:${Math.min(i*34,300)}ms" onclick="editAppSettings('${appjs}')">
        <div class="app-card-top">
          <div class="app-name-wrap"><div class="app-avatar">${esc(initials(name,a))}</div><div><div class="app-title">${nameEsc}</div><div class="app-sub">VER ${ver} · MIN ${minv}</div>${rawDiff?`<div class="app-raw-id">ID ${app}</div>`:''}</div></div>
          <span class="app-pill ${isClosed?'closed':'open'}"><span class="led"></span>${isClosed?'ĐÓNG':'MỞ'}</span>
        </div>
        <div class="app-metrics">
          <div class="metric-box"><div class="metric-lbl">Online</div><div class="metric-val green">${on}</div><div class="metric-bar"><span class="green" style="width:${Math.max(onp,on?8:0)}%"></span></div></div>
          <div class="metric-box"><div class="metric-lbl">Offline</div><div class="metric-val red">${off}</div><div class="metric-bar"><span class="red" style="width:${Math.max(offp,off?8:0)}%"></span></div></div>
          <div class="metric-box"><div class="metric-lbl">Total</div><div class="metric-val blue">${total}</div><div class="metric-bar"><span class="blue" style="width:${total?100:0}%"></span></div></div>
        </div>
        <div class="app-state-line"><span style="width:${isClosed?100:Math.max(onp,total?8:0)}%"></span></div>
        <div class="app-info-row"><span>Trạng thái</span><span class="app-flags"><span class="badge ${notice?'on':'gray'}">NOTICE ${notice?'ON':'OFF'}</span><span class="badge ${soft?'blue':'gray'}">UPDATE ${soft?'SOFT':'OFF'}</span><span class="badge ${force?'banned':'gray'}">${force?'FORCE':'NO FORCE'}</span>${isDev?'<span class="badge cyan">DEV</span>':''}</span></div>
        <div class="app-actions"><button class="btn ${isClosed?'success':'danger'}" onclick="event.stopPropagation();quickSetAppClosed('${appjs}',${isClosed?0:1})">${isClosed?'Mở app':'Đóng app'}</button></div>
      </div>`;
    }).join('');
  };

  const prevEdit = window.editAppSettings;
  if(typeof prevEdit==='function'){
    window.editAppSettings=function(appName){
      prevEdit.apply(this,arguments);
      ensureRenameFields();
      const nm=document.getElementById('app-display-name'); if(nm) nm.value=displayName(appName);
      const chip=document.getElementById('app-raw-id-chip'); if(chip) chip.textContent=safeText(appName).toUpperCase();
      const title=document.getElementById('appctl-edit-name'); if(title) title.textContent=displayName(appName);
      document.querySelectorAll('#appctl-grid .app-card').forEach(c=>c.classList.toggle('selected',c.dataset.app===appName));
    };
  }

  const prevLoad = window.loadAllAppSettings;
  if(typeof prevLoad==='function'){
    window.loadAllAppSettings=async function(){
      const r=await prevLoad.apply(this,arguments);
      renderAppSwitcher(); renderAppControl(); if(editApp) editAppSettings(editApp);
      return r;
    };
  }

  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',()=>setTimeout(()=>{ensureRenameFields(); renderAppSwitcher(); if(document.getElementById('appctl-grid')) renderAppControl(); if(editApp) editAppSettings(editApp);},160));
})();



/* FINAL APP ID + DISPLAY NAME SERVER PATCH */
(function(){
  const css = `
  #app-id-server-wrap{border:1px solid rgba(96,165,250,.14);background:linear-gradient(180deg,rgba(96,165,250,.055),rgba(15,23,42,.20));border-radius:16px;padding:12px;margin-bottom:8px}
  #app-id-server-wrap .id-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:minmax(0,1fr) minmax(0,1fr);gap:10px}
  #app-id-server-wrap .id-warning{margin-top:9px;font-family:var(--mono);font-size:9px;line-height:1.55;color:rgba(251,191,36,.82)}
  #save-app-display-name-server{border-color:rgba(34,211,238,.34)!important;background:rgba(34,211,238,.08)!important;color:#67e8f9!important}
  #rename-app-id-server{border-color:rgba(251,191,36,.34)!important;background:rgba(251,191,36,.08)!important;color:#fbbf24!important}
  .app-id-line{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:8px;letter-spacing:1px;color:rgba(148,163,184,.50);margin-top:4px;text-transform:uppercase;white-space:nowrap;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;max-width:220px}
  .app-btn .app-id-mini{font-size:7px;letter-spacing:1px;color:rgba(148,163,184,.48);margin-left:5px}
  .app-btn.dev-app-btn .app-id-mini{color:rgba(103,232,249,.58)}
  .app-card.dev-card .app-id-line{color:rgba(103,232,249,.62)}
  @media(max-width:720px){#app-id-server-wrap .id-grid{grid-template-columns:1fr}}
  `;
  document.getElementById('server-app-id-final-style')?.remove();
  const st=document.createElement('style'); st.id='server-app-id-final-style'; st.textContent=css; document.head.appendChild(st);

  function S(v){return String(v??'');}
  function E(v){return S(v).replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;').replace(/>/g,'&gt;').replace(/"/g,'&quot;');}
  function J(v){return S(v).replace(/\\/g,'\\\\').replace(/'/g,"\\'");}
  function cleanId(id){return S(id).trim().replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_]/g,'').slice(0,32);}
  function cleanName(name){return S(name).trim().replace(/\s+/g,' ').slice(0,48);}
  function appSettings(id){const d=(typeof allAppSettings!=='undefined'&&allAppSettings)||window.allAppSettings||{};return d[id]||{};}
  function appName(id){const st=appSettings(id);return cleanName(st.display_name)||cleanName(st.name)||S(id).toUpperCase();}
  function initials(name,id){const src=cleanName(name)||S(id);const p=src.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);if(p.length>=2)return (p[0][0]+p[1][0]).toUpperCase();return src.slice(0,2).toUpperCase();}
  function devAppId(){
    const data=(typeof allAppSettings!=='undefined'&&allAppSettings)||window.allAppSettings||{};
    for(const [id,st] of Object.entries(data)){ if(st && st.is_dev_app) return id; }
    const saved=localStorage.getItem('czDevAppId')||''; if(saved && (!Array.isArray(apps)||apps.includes(saved))) return saved;
    const list=Array.isArray(apps)?apps:[]; return list.find(a=>/dev|test/i.test(String(a)))||'';
  }
  window.getDevAppId = devAppId;
  window.setDevAppId = async function(appName){
    if(!appName)return;
    const res = await apiForApp(appName,{action:'setdevapp',admin:'mkadmin',target:appName});
    if(res && res.ok){ localStorage.setItem('czDevAppId',appName); toast(appName.toUpperCase()+' đã là app DEV','success'); await loadAllAppSettings(); renderAppSwitcher(); }
    else toast('Không lưu được app DEV','error');
  };

  window.renderAppSwitcher=function(){
    const c=document.getElementById('app-switcher'); if(!c)return;
    const dev=devAppId();
    const list=[...(apps||[])].sort((a,b)=>(a===dev?-1:b===dev?1:appName(a).localeCompare(appName(b))));
    c.innerHTML='';
    list.forEach(a=>{
      const isDev=a===dev; const label=appName(a); const raw=label.toLowerCase()!==S(a).toLowerCase();
      const b=document.createElement('button');
      b.className='app-btn'+(a===currentApp?' act':'')+(isDev?' dev-app-btn':'');
      b.innerHTML=(isDev?'<span class="dev-kicker">DEV</span>':'')+'<span>'+E(label)+'</span>'+(raw?'<span class="app-id-mini">'+E(a.toUpperCase())+'</span>':'');
      b.onclick=()=>switchApp(a); c.appendChild(b);
    });
  };

  window.renderAppControl=function(){
    const grid=document.getElementById('appctl-grid'); if(!grid)return;
    const data=(typeof allAppSettings!=='undefined'&&allAppSettings)||window.allAppSettings||{};
    const entries=Object.entries(data);
    let online=0,offline=0,closed=0,noticeOn=0;
    entries.forEach(([a,s])=>{const st=s.stats||{};online+=Number(st.online||0);offline+=Number(st.offline||0);if(s.closed)closed++;if(s.notice_enabled)noticeOn++;});
    const set=(id,v)=>{const e=document.getElementById(id); if(e)e.textContent=v;};
    set('appctl-online',online); set('appctl-offline',offline); set('appctl-closed',closed); set('appctl-noticeon',noticeOn);
    if(!entries.length){grid.innerHTML='<div class="es"><span class="ei">◌</span>Không có dữ liệu</div>';return;}
    const dev=devAppId();
    const sorted=entries.sort(([a],[b])=>(a===dev?-1:b===dev?1:appName(a).localeCompare(appName(b))));
    grid.innerHTML=sorted.map(([a,s],i)=>{
      const st=s.stats||{}; const total=Number(st.total||0); const on=Number(st.online||0); const off=Number(st.offline||0);
      const onp=total?Math.round(on/total*100):0; const offp=total?Math.round(off/total*100):0;
      const isClosed=!!s.closed, notice=!!s.notice_enabled, soft=!!(s.version_check_enabled||s.version_enabled), force=!!s.force_update_enabled, isDev=a===dev;
      const ver=E(s.app_version||s.latest_version||'1.0'), minv=E(s.min_required_version||'1.0');
      const label=appName(a); const raw=label.toLowerCase()!==S(a).toLowerCase(); const appjs=J(a);
      return `<div class="app-card ${isClosed?'closed':'open'} ${isDev?'dev-card':''} ${editApp===a?'selected':''}" data-app="${E(a)}" data-closed="${isClosed?'1':'0'}" data-notice="${notice?'1':'0'}" data-force="${force?'1':'0'}" style="animation-delay:${Math.min(i*34,300)}ms" onclick="editAppSettings('${appjs}')">
        <div class="app-card-top">
          <div class="app-name-wrap"><div class="app-avatar">${E(initials(label,a))}</div><div><div class="app-title">${E(label)}</div><div class="app-sub">VER ${ver} · MIN ${minv}</div>${raw?`<div class="app-id-line">APP ID ${E(a)}</div>`:''}</div></div>
          <span class="app-pill ${isClosed?'closed':'open'}"><span class="led"></span>${isClosed?'ĐÓNG':'MỞ'}</span>
        </div>
        <div class="app-metrics">
          <div class="metric-box"><div class="metric-lbl">Online</div><div class="metric-val green">${on}</div><div class="metric-bar"><span class="green" style="width:${Math.max(onp,on?8:0)}%"></span></div></div>
          <div class="metric-box"><div class="metric-lbl">Offline</div><div class="metric-val red">${off}</div><div class="metric-bar"><span class="red" style="width:${Math.max(offp,off?8:0)}%"></span></div></div>
          <div class="metric-box"><div class="metric-lbl">Total</div><div class="metric-val blue">${total}</div><div class="metric-bar"><span class="blue" style="width:${total?100:0}%"></span></div></div>
        </div>
        <div class="app-state-line"><span style="width:${isClosed?100:Math.max(onp,total?8:0)}%"></span></div>
        <div class="app-info-row"><span>Trạng thái</span><span class="app-flags"><span class="badge ${notice?'on':'gray'}">NOTICE ${notice?'ON':'OFF'}</span><span class="badge ${soft?'blue':'gray'}">UPDATE ${soft?'SOFT':'OFF'}</span><span class="badge ${force?'banned':'gray'}">${force?'FORCE':'NO FORCE'}</span>${isDev?'<span class="badge cyan">DEV</span>':''}</span></div>
        <div class="app-actions"><button class="btn ${isClosed?'success':'danger'}" onclick="event.stopPropagation();quickSetAppClosed('${appjs}',${isClosed?0:1})">${isClosed?'Mở app':'Đóng app'}</button></div>
      </div>`;
    }).join('');
  };

  function ensureAppIdFields(){
    const sec=document.querySelector('#appctl-sec-basic .form-grid'); if(!sec)return;
    document.getElementById('app-display-name-wrap')?.remove();
    if(document.getElementById('app-id-server-wrap')) return;
    const wrap=document.createElement('div'); wrap.className='form-field full'; wrap.id='app-id-server-wrap';
    wrap.innerHTML=`<label>Định danh app</label><div class="id-grid"><input class="inp" id="app-server-id" type="text" placeholder="App ID thật, ví dụ svrvn"><input class="inp" id="app-display-name-server" type="text" placeholder="Tên hiển thị, ví dụ Server Việt Nam"></div><div class="id-warning">Tên hiển thị chỉ đổi giao diện. App ID thật sẽ đổi key server và migrate file JSON theo app, nên hãy đổi cẩn thận.</div>`;
    sec.insertBefore(wrap,sec.firstChild);
    const actions=document.querySelector('#appctl-sec-basic .form-actions');
    if(actions && !document.getElementById('save-app-display-name-server')){
      const save=document.createElement('button'); save.type='button'; save.id='save-app-display-name-server'; save.className='btn action-btn'; save.textContent='Lưu tên hiển thị'; save.onclick=()=>saveAppIdentity(false);
      const rename=document.createElement('button'); rename.type='button'; rename.id='rename-app-id-server'; rename.className='btn action-btn'; rename.textContent='Đổi App ID'; rename.onclick=()=>saveAppIdentity(true);
      actions.insertBefore(rename,actions.firstChild); actions.insertBefore(save,actions.firstChild);
    }
  }
  async function saveAppIdentity(allowIdChange){
    if(!editApp)return;
    const oldId=editApp;
    const newId=cleanId(document.getElementById('app-server-id')?.value||oldId) || oldId;
    const name=cleanName(document.getElementById('app-display-name-server')?.value||appName(oldId));
    if(newId!==oldId && !allowIdChange){ toast('Muốn đổi App ID thật thì bấm nút Đổi App ID','warn'); return; }
    const run=async()=>{
      const res=await apiForApp(oldId,{action:'renameapp',admin:'mkadmin',new_id:newId,display_name:name});
      if(res&&res.ok&&res.data&&res.data.ok){
        if(Array.isArray(res.data.apps)) apps=res.data.apps;
        currentApp = (currentApp===oldId)?res.data.app:currentApp;
        editApp = res.data.app;
        renderAppSwitcher();
        await loadAllAppSettings();
        toast(newId!==oldId?'Đã đổi App ID và migrate dữ liệu':'Đã lưu tên hiển thị','success');
      } else toast('Lỗi lưu định danh app: '+(res?res.raw:'network'),'error');
    };
    if(newId!==oldId){ confirm2('Đổi App ID thật?',`Đổi ${oldId} thành ${newId}. Server sẽ migrate file dữ liệu JSON theo app. Tiếp tục?`,run); }
    else await run();
  }
  window.saveAppIdentity=saveAppIdentity;

  const prevEdit=window.editAppSettings;
  if(typeof prevEdit==='function'){
    window.editAppSettings=function(appName){
      prevEdit.apply(this,arguments);
      ensureAppIdFields();
      const id=document.getElementById('app-server-id'); if(id) id.value=appName;
      const nm=document.getElementById('app-display-name-server'); if(nm) nm.value=appName?appName in ((typeof allAppSettings!=='undefined'&&allAppSettings)||{}) ? appName in ((typeof allAppSettings!=='undefined'&&allAppSettings)||{}) && appName : appName : '';
      if(nm) nm.value=appName?appName in ((typeof allAppSettings!=='undefined'&&allAppSettings)||{}) ? appName in ((typeof allAppSettings!=='undefined'&&allAppSettings)||{}) && appName(appName) : appName : '';
    };
  }
  // Fix the wrapper above with a safer version after it is installed.
  if(typeof prevEdit==='function'){
    window.editAppSettings=function(appName){
      prevEdit.apply(this,arguments);
      ensureAppIdFields();
      const id=document.getElementById('app-server-id'); if(id) id.value=appName;
      const nm=document.getElementById('app-display-name-server'); if(nm) nm.value=appName ? appNameFromSettings(appName) : '';
      const title=document.getElementById('appctl-edit-name'); if(title) title.textContent=appNameFromSettings(appName);
      document.querySelectorAll('#appctl-grid .app-card').forEach(c=>c.classList.toggle('selected',c.dataset.app===appName));
    };
  }
  function appNameFromSettings(id){return appName(id);}

  const prevLoad=window.loadAllAppSettings;
  if(typeof prevLoad==='function'){
    window.loadAllAppSettings=async function(){const r=await prevLoad.apply(this,arguments);renderAppSwitcher();renderAppControl();if(editApp)editAppSettings(editApp);return r;};
  }
  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',()=>setTimeout(()=>{ensureAppIdFields();renderAppSwitcher();if(document.getElementById('appctl-grid'))renderAppControl();if(editApp)editAppSettings(editApp);},220));
})();

  `;
  document.getElementById('basic-final-compact-polish')?.remove();
  const st=document.createElement('style'); st.id='basic-final-compact-polish'; st.textContent=css; document.head.appendChild(st);

  function getState(){
    const data=(typeof allAppSettings!=='undefined'&&allAppSettings)||window.allAppSettings||{};
    return (editApp && data && data[editApp]) ? data[editApp] : {};
  }
  function label(){
    try{ return (typeof appNameFromSettings==='function' && editApp) ? appNameFromSettings(editApp) : (editApp||'').toUpperCase(); }
    catch{ return (editApp||'').toUpperCase(); }
  }
  async function saveIdentityAndToken(){
    if(!editApp) return;
    if(typeof saveAppIdentity==='function') await saveAppIdentity(false);
    if(typeof saveAppTokenForEdit==='function') await saveAppTokenForEdit();
  }
  window.saveIdentityAndToken = saveIdentityAndToken;

  function buildBasicConsole(){
    const sec=document.getElementById('appctl-sec-basic'); if(!sec) return;
    let box=document.getElementById('basic-console');
    if(!box){
      box=document.createElement('div'); box.id='basic-console'; box.className='basic-console';
      sec.appendChild(box);
    }
    const s=getState(); const closed=!!s.closed; const isDev=(typeof getDevAppId==='function' && editApp===getDevAppId());
    box.innerHTML=`
      <div class="basic-console-head">
        <div><div class="basic-console-title">Điều khiển nhanh</div><div class="basic-console-sub">${editApp ? label()+' · '+editApp : 'Chọn app để thao tác'}</div></div>
        <div style="display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap;justify-content:flex-end">
          <span class="basic-status-pill ${closed?'closed':'open'}"><span class="dot"></span>${closed?'ĐÓNG':'MỞ'}</span>
          ${isDev?'<span class="basic-status-pill dev"><span class="dot"></span>DEV</span>':''}
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="basic-action-grid">
        <button type="button" class="btn primary-save" onclick="saveIdentityAndToken()">Lưu cấu hình cơ bản</button>
        <button type="button" class="btn ${closed?'state-open':'state-close'}" onclick="setAppClosedForEdit(${closed?0:1})">${closed?'Mở app':'Đóng app'}</button>
        <button type="button" class="btn dev-action" onclick="setDevAppId(editApp)">${isDev?'Đang là app DEV':'Đặt làm app DEV'}</button>
      </div>
      <details class="basic-advanced">
        <summary>Tác vụ nâng cao</summary>
        <div class="basic-advanced-body">
          <button type="button" class="btn warn" onclick="confirmResetAppTokenForEdit()">Reset token</button>
          <button type="button" class="btn warn" onclick="saveAppIdentity(true)">Đổi App ID</button>
          <div class="basic-danger-note">Đổi App ID sẽ migrate key/file JSON trên server. Chỉ dùng khi thật sự muốn đổi ID app.</div>
        </div>
      </details>`;
  }
  const prevEdit=window.editAppSettings;
  if(typeof prevEdit==='function'){
    window.editAppSettings=function(){
      const r=prevEdit.apply(this,arguments);
      setTimeout(buildBasicConsole,0);
      return r;
    };
  }
  const prevLoad=window.loadAllAppSettings;
  if(typeof prevLoad==='function'){
    window.loadAllAppSettings=async function(){
      const r=await prevLoad.apply(this,arguments);
      setTimeout(buildBasicConsole,0);
      return r;
    };
  }
  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',()=>setTimeout(buildBasicConsole,260));
})();

<script id="v10-light-pro-js">
(function(){
  function ready(fn){document.readyState==='loading'?document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',fn):fn();}
  ready(function(){
    document.body.classList.add('v10-rebuild');
    var title=document.querySelector('.brand-title'); if(title) title.textContent='CONTROL OPS';
    var sub=document.querySelector('.brand-sub'); if(sub) sub.textContent='Lean Admin Console';

    // Wrap nav icons once, for the new rail layout.
    document.querySelectorAll('.nav-item').forEach(function(item){
      var svg=item.querySelector(':scope > svg');
      if(svg && !svg.parentElement.classList.contains('nav-icon-wrap')){
        var wrap=document.createElement('span'); wrap.className='nav-icon-wrap';
        item.insertBefore(wrap,svg); wrap.appendChild(svg);
      }
    });

    // Add a small command dock. Lightweight: no polling, no canvas, no heavy animation.
    if(!document.querySelector('.v10-dock')){
      var dock=document.createElement('div');
      dock.className='v10-dock';
      dock.innerHTML='<button class="accent" data-act="reload">Reload</button><button data-act="compact">Compact</button><button data-act="focus">Focus</button><button data-act="top">Top</button>';
      document.body.appendChild(dock);
      dock.addEventListener('click',function(e){
        var b=e.target.closest('button'); if(!b) return;
        var act=b.getAttribute('data-act');
        if(act==='reload' && typeof window.reloadCurrentPage==='function') window.reloadCurrentPage();
        if(act==='compact') document.body.classList.toggle('v10-compact');
        if(act==='focus') document.body.classList.toggle('v10-focus');
        if(act==='top'){ var m=document.querySelector('main'); if(m) m.scrollTo({top:0,behavior:'smooth'}); }
      });
    }

    // Keyboard helpers: Alt+C compact, Alt+F focus, Alt+R reload.
    window.addEventListener('keydown',function(e){
      if(!e.altKey) return;
      var k=e.key.toLowerCase();
      if(k==='c'){e.preventDefault();document.body.classList.toggle('v10-compact');}
      if(k==='f'){e.preventDefault();document.body.classList.toggle('v10-focus');}
      if(k==='r'){e.preventDefault(); if(typeof window.reloadCurrentPage==='function') window.reloadCurrentPage();}
    });

    // Lightweight App Control filter, injected only if App Control exists.
    function ensureLiteFilter(){
      var grid=document.getElementById('appctl-grid'); if(!grid || document.getElementById('v10-app-filter')) return;
      var box=document.createElement('div');
      box.id='v10-app-filter';
      box.className='qbar';
      box.style.marginBottom='14px';
      box.innerHTML='<label>APP BOARD</label><div class="qrow"><input class="inp grow" id="v10-app-search" placeholder="Tìm app nhanh..." autocomplete="off"><select class="sel" id="v10-app-state"><option value="all">Tất cả</option><option value="open">Đang mở</option><option value="closed">Đang đóng</option><option value="notice">Notice ON</option><option value="force">Force update</option></select></div>';
      grid.parentElement.insertBefore(box,grid);
      function apply(){
        var q=(document.getElementById('v10-app-search')?.value||'').toLowerCase().trim();
        var st=document.getElementById('v10-app-state')?.value||'all';
        var visible=0;
        grid.querySelectorAll('.app-card').forEach(function(card){
          var txt=card.textContent.toLowerCase();
          var ok=!q||txt.includes(q)||(card.dataset.app||'').toLowerCase().includes(q);
          if(st==='open') ok=ok&&card.dataset.closed==='0';
          if(st==='closed') ok=ok&&card.dataset.closed==='1';
          if(st==='notice') ok=ok&&card.dataset.notice==='1';
          if(st==='force') ok=ok&&card.dataset.force==='1';
          card.classList.toggle('is-hidden',!ok); if(ok) visible++;
        });
        grid.classList.toggle('filtered-empty',visible===0);
      }
      box.addEventListener('input',apply); box.addEventListener('change',apply);
    }
    ensureLiteFilter();
    new MutationObserver(ensureLiteFilter).observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});
  });
})();
</script>


<script id="v11-smooth-mobile-js">
(function(){
  function ready(fn){document.readyState==='loading'?document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',fn):fn();}
  ready(function(){
    document.body.classList.add('v11-ready');
    var connectBtn=document.querySelector('#topbar.cmdbar>button[onclick*="openServerModal"]'); if(connectBtn) connectBtn.remove();
    var reloadBtn=document.querySelector('#topbar.cmdbar>button[onclick*="reloadCurrentPage"]'); if(reloadBtn) reloadBtn.remove();
    var chip=document.getElementById('server-chip');
    if(chip){
      chip.setAttribute('title','Click để nhập hoặc đổi URL server');
      chip.setAttribute('role','button');
      chip.setAttribute('tabindex','0');
      chip.addEventListener('keydown',function(e){if(e.key==='Enter'||e.key===' '){e.preventDefault(); if(typeof openServerModal==='function') openServerModal();}});
    }
    // Rename dock reload to avoid confusing it with per-page data buttons.
    var dockReload=document.querySelector('.v10-dock button[data-act="reload"]');
    if(dockReload) dockReload.textContent='Reload';
    // Ensure last focused control is not hidden behind the mobile dock.
    document.addEventListener('focusin',function(e){
      if(!window.matchMedia('(max-width:760px)').matches) return;
      var main=document.querySelector('main'); if(!main || !main.contains(e.target)) return;
      setTimeout(function(){try{e.target.scrollIntoView({block:'center',inline:'nearest',behavior:'smooth'});}catch(_){}} ,60);
    });
  });
})();
</script>

<script id="v15-final-admin-fixes-js">
(function(){
  function S(v){return String(v??'');}
  function E(v){return S(v).replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;').replace(/>/g,'&gt;').replace(/"/g,'&quot;');}
  function J(v){return S(v).replace(/\\/g,'\\\\').replace(/'/g,"\\'");}
  function state(){return (typeof allAppSettings!=='undefined'&&allAppSettings)||window.allAppSettings||{};}
  function labelOf(id){const s=state()[id]||{};return S(s.display_name||s.name||id).trim()||S(id).toUpperCase();}
  function cacheLabels(){try{const m={};Object.keys(state()).forEach(k=>m[k]=labelOf(k));localStorage.setItem('czAppLabels',JSON.stringify(m));}catch(e){}}
  function cachedLabel(id){try{const m=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('czAppLabels')||'{}');return m[id]||labelOf(id);}catch(e){return labelOf(id);}}
  function initials(name,id){const p=S(name||id).trim().split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);return (p.length>1?(p[0][0]+p[1][0]):S(name||id).slice(0,2)).toUpperCase();}

  function hideReloadButtons(){
    document.querySelectorAll('.ph .acts .btn').forEach(b=>{if(/tải\s*lại/i.test(b.textContent||''))b.classList.add('v15-hide-reload');});
  }
  function tidyTop(){
    const title=document.querySelector('.brand-title'); if(title) title.textContent='CLAZY OPS';
    const sub=document.querySelector('.brand-sub'); if(sub) sub.textContent='Admin Control';
    hideReloadButtons();
  }

  const oldLoadApps=window.loadApps;
  if(typeof oldLoadApps==='function'){
    window.loadApps=async function(){
      const res=await api({action:'getapps',admin:'mkadmin'});
      if(res&&res.ok&&Array.isArray(res.data)&&res.data.length){apps=res.data;} else apps=['svrvn','svrtw','hong','svrtest'];
      if(!apps.includes(currentApp)) currentApp=apps[0];
      const meta=await api({action:'listappsettings',admin:'mkadmin'});
      if(meta&&meta.ok&&meta.data&&typeof meta.data==='object'){allAppSettings=meta.data;window.allAppSettings=meta.data;cacheLabels();}
      renderAppSwitcher();
      if(typeof reloadCurrentPage==='function') reloadCurrentPage(); else reloadAll();
    };
  }

  window.renderAppSwitcher=function(){
    const c=document.getElementById('app-switcher'); if(!c)return;
    const data=state();
    const dev=(typeof getDevAppId==='function'&&getDevAppId())||Object.keys(data).find(k=>data[k]&&data[k].is_dev_app)||'';
    const list=[...(apps||[])].sort((a,b)=>(a===dev?-1:b===dev?1:cachedLabel(a).localeCompare(cachedLabel(b))));
    c.innerHTML='';
    list.forEach(a=>{
      const isDev=a===dev; const label=cachedLabel(a); const raw=label.toLowerCase()!==S(a).toLowerCase();
      const b=document.createElement('button'); b.className='app-btn'+(a===currentApp?' act':'')+(isDev?' dev-app-btn':'');
      b.innerHTML=(isDev?'<span class="dev-kicker">DEV</span>':'')+'<span>'+E(label)+'</span>'+(raw?'<span class="app-id-mini">'+E(S(a).toUpperCase())+'</span>':'');
      b.onclick=()=>switchApp(a); c.appendChild(b);
    });
  };

  window.renderAppControl=function(){
    const grid=document.getElementById('appctl-grid'); if(!grid)return;
    const data=state(); const entries=Object.entries(data); cacheLabels();
    let online=0,offline=0,closed=0,noticeOn=0;
    entries.forEach(([a,s])=>{const st=s.stats||{};online+=Number(st.online||0);offline+=Number(st.offline||0);if(s.closed)closed++;if(s.notice_enabled)noticeOn++;});
    const set=(id,v)=>{const e=document.getElementById(id);if(e)e.textContent=v;};set('appctl-online',online);set('appctl-offline',offline);set('appctl-closed',closed);set('appctl-noticeon',noticeOn);
    if(!entries.length){grid.innerHTML='<div class="es"><span class="ei">◌</span>Không có dữ liệu app</div>';return;}
    const dev=(typeof getDevAppId==='function'&&getDevAppId())||entries.find(([_,s])=>s&&s.is_dev_app)?.[0]||'';
    entries.sort(([a],[b])=>(a===dev?-1:b===dev?1:labelOf(a).localeCompare(labelOf(b))));
    grid.innerHTML=entries.map(([a,s])=>{
      const st=s.stats||{};const total=Number(st.total||0),on=Number(st.online||0),off=Number(st.offline||0);const onp=total?Math.round(on/total*100):0;const offp=total?Math.round(off/total*100):0;
      const isClosed=!!s.closed,notice=!!s.notice_enabled,soft=!!(s.version_check_enabled||s.version_enabled),force=!!s.force_update_enabled,isDev=a===dev;
      const label=labelOf(a),raw=label.toLowerCase()!==S(a).toLowerCase(),appjs=J(a);
      return `<div class="app-card ${isClosed?'closed':'open'} ${isDev?'dev-card':''} ${editApp===a?'selected':''}" data-app="${E(a)}" data-closed="${isClosed?'1':'0'}" data-notice="${notice?'1':'0'}" data-force="${force?'1':'0'}" onclick="editAppSettings('${appjs}')">
        <div class="app-card-top"><div class="app-name-wrap"><div class="app-avatar">${E(initials(label,a))}</div><div><div class="app-title" title="${E(label)}">${E(label)}</div><div class="app-sub">VER ${E(s.app_version||s.latest_version||'1.0')} · MIN ${E(s.min_required_version||'1.0')}</div>${raw?`<div class="app-id-line">APP ID ${E(a)}</div>`:''}</div></div><span class="app-pill ${isClosed?'closed':'open'}"><span class="led"></span>${isClosed?'ĐÓNG':'MỞ'}</span></div>
        <div class="app-metrics"><div class="metric-box"><div class="metric-lbl">Online</div><div class="metric-val green">${on}</div><div class="metric-bar"><span class="green" style="width:${Math.max(onp,on?8:0)}%"></span></div></div><div class="metric-box"><div class="metric-lbl">Offline</div><div class="metric-val red">${off}</div><div class="metric-bar"><span class="red" style="width:${Math.max(offp,off?8:0)}%"></span></div></div><div class="metric-box"><div class="metric-lbl">Total</div><div class="metric-val blue">${total}</div><div class="metric-bar"><span class="blue" style="width:${total?100:0}%"></span></div></div></div>
        <div class="app-state-line"><span style="width:${isClosed?100:Math.max(onp,total?8:0)}%"></span></div><div class="app-info-row"><span>Trạng thái</span><span class="app-flags"><span class="badge ${notice?'on':'gray'}">NOTICE ${notice?'ON':'OFF'}</span><span class="badge ${soft?'blue':'gray'}">UPDATE ${soft?'SOFT':'OFF'}</span><span class="badge ${force?'banned':'gray'}">${force?'FORCE':'NO FORCE'}</span>${isDev?'<span class="badge cyan">DEV</span>':''}</span></div>
        <div class="app-actions"><button class="btn ${isClosed?'success':'danger'}" onclick="event.stopPropagation();quickSetAppClosed('${appjs}',${isClosed?0:1})">${isClosed?'Mở app':'Đóng app'}</button></div></div>`;
    }).join('');
  };

  const prevEdit=window.editAppSettings;
  if(typeof prevEdit==='function'){
    window.editAppSettings=function(appName){const r=prevEdit.apply(this,arguments);setTimeout(()=>{const nm=document.getElementById('app-display-name-server');if(nm)nm.value=labelOf(appName);const title=document.getElementById('appctl-edit-name');if(title)title.textContent=labelOf(appName);document.querySelectorAll('#appctl-grid .app-card').forEach(c=>c.classList.toggle('selected',c.dataset.app===appName));},0);return r;};
  }

  // Bigger JSON editor + wrapper mode.
  const oldToggle=window.toggleUrlJson;
  window.toggleUrlJson=function(force){
    const ta=document.getElementById('url-json'),acts=document.getElementById('url-json-actions'),shell=document.querySelector('.url-shell'); if(!ta||!acts)return;
    const show=force===undefined?ta.style.display==='none'||getComputedStyle(ta).display==='none':!!force;
    if(show){ta.value=JSON.stringify(currentUrlMap||{},null,2);ta.style.display='block';acts.style.display='flex';shell?.classList.add('json-mode');setTimeout(()=>ta.focus(),30);}else{ta.style.display='none';acts.style.display='none';shell?.classList.remove('json-mode');}
  };

  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',()=>{tidyTop();setTimeout(()=>{renderAppSwitcher();hideReloadButtons();},120);});
  window.addEventListener('load',()=>{tidyTop();setTimeout(()=>{hideReloadButtons();if(document.getElementById('appctl-grid'))renderAppControl();},350);});
  const mo=new MutationObserver(()=>hideReloadButtons()); mo.observe(document.documentElement,{childList:true,subtree:true});
})();
</script>

<script id="v15-server-config-panel">
(function(){
  function esc(v){return String(v??'').replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;').replace(/>/g,'&gt;').replace(/"/g,'&quot;');}
  function ensureServerConfigPage(){
    if(!document.getElementById('ni-servercfg')){
      const dedup=document.getElementById('ni-dedup');
      const item=document.createElement('div'); item.className='nav-item'; item.id='ni-servercfg'; item.onclick=function(){goPage('servercfg',this)};
      item.innerHTML='<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8"><path d="M12 3l8 4v5c0 5-3.3 8-8 9-4.7-1-8-4-8-9V7l8-4z"/><path d="M9 12l2 2 4-5"/></svg>Server Config';
      (dedup?.parentNode||document.querySelector('.nav'))?.insertBefore(item,dedup?.nextSibling||null);
    }
    if(!document.getElementById('page-servercfg')){
      const main=document.querySelector('main'); if(!main)return;
      const page=document.createElement('div'); page.className='page'; page.id='page-servercfg';
      page.innerHTML=`<div class="ph"><h2>Server Config <span class="atag blue" id="tag-servercfg">${esc((window.currentApp||'').toUpperCase())}</span></h2><div class="acts"><button class="btn success" onclick="saveServerConfigPanel()">Lưu cấu hình</button></div></div>
      <div class="server-config-grid">
        <div class="server-config-card"><h3>◎ Ticket / Rate limit</h3>
          <div class="form-field"><label>Rate limit mỗi device / ngày</label><input class="inp" id="sc-rate" type="number" min="1" max="100000" placeholder="500"></div>
          <div class="form-field"><label>Ticket TTL giây</label><input class="inp" id="sc-ttl" type="number" min="60" max="86400" placeholder="1800"></div>
          <div class="form-field"><label>Online window giây</label><input class="inp" id="sc-online" type="number" min="30" max="3600" placeholder="120"></div>
          <div class="form-field"><label>Limit tải URL map / IP / phút</label><input class="inp" id="sc-iplimit" type="number" min="1" max="1000" placeholder="10"></div>
        </div>
        <div class="server-config-card"><h3>◎ Whitelist bỏ qua verify hash</h3>
          <div class="hint-line" style="margin-bottom:10px">Mỗi dòng một Device ID. Các ID trong whitelist sẽ bỏ qua kiểm tra hash/tamper trong action ticket.</div>
          <textarea class="textarea-clean" id="sc-whitelist" spellcheck="false" placeholder="20CD45AE-EDA5-0D82-7F70-77041F935950"></textarea>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="cc" style="margin-top:14px"><h3>Gợi ý chức năng có thể quản lý qua panel</h3><p style="margin:0;line-height:1.8">Đã thêm được các mục không cần mở PHP: rate limit, TTL ticket, online window, limit tải URL map, whitelist device. Các mục đã có sẵn trong panel/server: App ID, tên hiển thị, token, đóng/mở app, notice, version/update, bảo trì, URL map, dev app, hash learn, token blacklist, dedup.</p></div>`;
      main.appendChild(page);
    }
  }
  window.loadServerConfigPanel=async function(){
    const tag=document.getElementById('tag-servercfg'); if(tag)tag.textContent=(currentApp||'').toUpperCase();
    const res=await api({action:'getserverconfig',admin:'mkadmin'});
    if(!res||!res.ok||!res.data||!res.data.config){toast('Không tải được Server Config','error');return;}
    const c=res.data.config;
    document.getElementById('sc-rate').value=c.rate_limit_per_day??500;
    document.getElementById('sc-ttl').value=c.ticket_ttl_sec??1800;
    document.getElementById('sc-online').value=c.online_window_sec??120;
    document.getElementById('sc-iplimit').value=c.ip_urlmap_limit_per_min??10;
    document.getElementById('sc-whitelist').value=(c.whitelist_device_ids||[]).join('\n');
  };
  window.saveServerConfigPanel=async function(){
    const res=await api({action:'setserverconfig',admin:'mkadmin',rate_limit_per_day:document.getElementById('sc-rate').value,ticket_ttl_sec:document.getElementById('sc-ttl').value,online_window_sec:document.getElementById('sc-online').value,ip_urlmap_limit_per_min:document.getElementById('sc-iplimit').value,whitelist_device_ids:document.getElementById('sc-whitelist').value});
    if(res&&res.ok&&res.data&&res.data.ok){toast('Đã lưu Server Config','success');loadServerConfigPanel();if(typeof loadAllAppSettings==='function')loadAllAppSettings();}
    else toast('Lỗi lưu Server Config','error');
  };
  const prevGo=window.goPage;
  window.goPage=function(name,el){ensureServerConfigPage();const r=prevGo.apply(this,arguments);if(name==='servercfg')setTimeout(loadServerConfigPanel,40);return r;};
  const prevSwitch=window.switchApp;
  if(typeof prevSwitch==='function') window.switchApp=function(a){const r=prevSwitch.apply(this,arguments);if(document.getElementById('page-servercfg')?.classList.contains('act'))setTimeout(loadServerConfigPanel,120);return r;};
  const prevReload=window.reloadCurrentPage;
  window.reloadCurrentPage=function(){const active=document.querySelector('.page.act')?.id?.replace('page-','');if(active==='servercfg')return loadServerConfigPanel();return prevReload?prevReload.apply(this,arguments):undefined;};
  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',()=>setTimeout(ensureServerConfigPage,180));
  window.addEventListener('load',()=>setTimeout(ensureServerConfigPage,250));
})();
</script>


<script id="v12-caption-toggle-save-polish-js">
(function(){
  const CAPTIONS={
    devices:'Thống kê tất cả thiết bị đã đăng ký của app đang chọn, trạng thái active/ban.',
    banlist:'Danh sách thiết bị đang bị chặn, lý do ban và thao tác mở chặn theo từng app.',
    banstats:'Thống kê lý do vi phạm, số lượng ban và biểu đồ theo dữ liệu hiện tại.',
    hash:'Quản lý learn mode, bật/tắt từng loại hash và reset cấu hình xác thực.',
    devmode:'Bật/tắt chế độ dev, điều khiển flag dev và reset keychain khi cần test.',
    appctl:'Quản lý app, token, notice, version, bảo trì và trạng thái online/offline tập trung.',
    urlmap:'Sửa link và text động theo từng app ngay trên panel, không cần mở PHP.',
    tokenbl:'Quản lý token blacklist, trạng thái tamper và xoá chặn tầng token.',
    dedup:'Dọn thiết bị trùng lặp, giữ fingerprint chuẩn và làm sạch dữ liệu cũ.'
  };

  function applyCaptions(){
    Object.entries(CAPTIONS).forEach(([name,caption])=>{
      const h=document.querySelector('#page-'+name+' .ph h2');
      if(h) h.setAttribute('data-caption',caption);
    });
  }

  function ensureAppCtlStatus(){
    const editor=document.getElementById('appctl-editor');
    if(!editor) return null;
    let box=document.getElementById('appctl-save-status');
    if(box) return box;
    box=document.createElement('div');
    box.id='appctl-save-status';
    box.innerHTML='<span class="status-dot"></span><span class="status-text">Sẵn sàng lưu cấu hình.</span>';
    const head=editor.querySelector('.editor-head');
    if(head && head.parentNode) head.parentNode.insertBefore(box,head.nextSibling);
    else editor.insertBefore(box,editor.firstChild);
    return box;
  }

  let statusTimer=null;
  function setAppCtlStatus(type,msg,persist){
    const box=ensureAppCtlStatus(); if(!box) return;
    box.className='show '+(type||'');
    const txt=box.querySelector('.status-text');
    if(txt) txt.textContent=msg;
    clearTimeout(statusTimer);
    if(!persist){
      statusTimer=setTimeout(()=>{box.className='';}, type==='error'?5200:3600);
    }
  }

  const ACTION_LABEL={
    setapptoken:'TOKEN',
    resetapptoken:'RESET TOKEN',
    setmaintenance:'BẢO TRÌ',
    setversion:'VERSION',
    setnotice:'NOTICE',
    setappclosed:'TRẠNG THÁI APP',
    renameapp:'ĐỊNH DANH APP'
  };

  function patchApiForApp(){
    if(window.__v12ApiForAppPatched) return;
    if(typeof window.apiForApp!=='function') return;
    window.__v12ApiForAppPatched=true;
    const old=window.apiForApp;
    window.apiForApp=async function(appName,params){
      const action=params && params.action;
      const label=ACTION_LABEL[action];
      if(label) setAppCtlStatus('saving','Đang lưu '+label+' cho '+String(appName||'APP').toUpperCase()+'...',true);
      let res;
      try{
        res=await old.apply(this,arguments);
      }catch(e){
        if(label) setAppCtlStatus('error','Lưu '+label+' thất bại: '+e.message);
        throw e;
      }
      if(label){
        const ok=!!(res&&res.ok&&!(res.data&&res.data.ok===false));
        if(ok) setAppCtlStatus('success','Đã lưu '+label+' cho '+String(appName||'APP').toUpperCase()+'.');
        else setAppCtlStatus('error','Lưu '+label+' thất bại: '+(res?(res.raw||('HTTP '+res.status)):'network'));
      }
      return res;
    };
  }

  const SWITCHES=[
    ['notice-enabled','BẬT NOTICE','TẮT NOTICE','Bật để client nhận notice độc lập theo app.'],
    ['version-enabled','BẬT UPDATE','TẮT UPDATE','Bật để hiện thông báo update mềm cho version mới.'],
    ['version-force','BẮT BUỘC','KHÔNG ÉP','Chỉ ép update khi client thấp hơn Min required.']
  ];

  function refreshSwitch(sw,sel,onLabel,offLabel){
    const on=String(sel.value)==='1';
    sw.classList.toggle('on',on);
    sw.classList.toggle('off',!on);
    const a=sw.querySelector('.smart-option.on .smart-label');
    const b=sw.querySelector('.smart-option.off .smart-label');
    if(a) a.textContent=onLabel;
    if(b) b.textContent=offLabel;
    sw.setAttribute('aria-pressed',on?'true':'false');
  }

  function installSwitch(id,onLabel,offLabel,hint){
    const sel=document.getElementById(id);
    if(!sel || sel.dataset.smartSwitchInstalled==='1') return;
    sel.dataset.smartSwitchInstalled='1';
    sel.classList.add('smart-hidden');
    const field=sel.closest('.form-field');
    if(field) field.classList.add('has-smart-switch');

    const sw=document.createElement('button');
    sw.type='button';
    sw.className='smart-switch';
    sw.innerHTML='<span class="smart-bg"></span><span class="smart-option on"><span class="mini-led"></span><span class="smart-label"></span></span><span class="smart-option off"><span class="mini-led"></span><span class="smart-label"></span></span>';
    sw.onclick=()=>{
      sel.value=String(sel.value)==='1'?'0':'1';
      sel.dispatchEvent(new Event('change',{bubbles:true}));
      refreshSwitch(sw,sel,onLabel,offLabel);
    };
    sel.insertAdjacentElement('afterend',sw);
    if(hint){
      const small=document.createElement('div');
      small.className='smart-switch-hint';
      small.textContent=hint;
      sw.insertAdjacentElement('afterend',small);
    }
    refreshSwitch(sw,sel,onLabel,offLabel);
  }

  function installSwitches(){
    SWITCHES.forEach(args=>installSwitch.apply(null,args));
    refreshAllSwitches();
  }

  function refreshAllSwitches(){
    SWITCHES.forEach(([id,onLabel,offLabel])=>{
      const sel=document.getElementById(id);
      if(!sel) return;
      const sw=sel.parentNode && sel.parentNode.querySelector('.smart-switch');
      if(sw) refreshSwitch(sw,sel,onLabel,offLabel);
    });
  }

  function patchEditRefresh(){
    if(window.__v12EditRefreshPatched) return;
    if(typeof window.editAppSettings!=='function') return;
    window.__v12EditRefreshPatched=true;
    const old=window.editAppSettings;
    window.editAppSettings=function(){
      const r=old.apply(this,arguments);
      setTimeout(()=>{installSwitches();refreshAllSwitches();ensureAppCtlStatus();},0);
      return r;
    };
  }

  function patchSectionTabs(){
    if(window.__v12SectionPatched) return;
    if(typeof window.showAppCtlSection!=='function') return;
    window.__v12SectionPatched=true;
    const old=window.showAppCtlSection;
    window.showAppCtlSection=function(){
      const r=old.apply(this,arguments);
      setTimeout(()=>{installSwitches();refreshAllSwitches();},0);
      return r;
    };
  }

  function init(){
    applyCaptions();
    ensureAppCtlStatus();
    installSwitches();
    patchApiForApp();
    patchEditRefresh();
    patchSectionTabs();
  }

  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',()=>setTimeout(init,80));
  window.addEventListener('load',()=>setTimeout(init,220));
  window.v12PanelPolishRefresh=init;
})();
</script>

<script id="v18-appctl-feedback-toggle-polish-js">
(function(){
  function ensureFloatingStatus(){
    let el=document.getElementById('v18-save-toast');
    if(el) return el;
    el=document.createElement('div');
    el.id='v18-save-toast';
    el.innerHTML='<div class="v18-ico">•</div><div><div class="v18-title">Đang xử lý</div><div class="v18-msg">Vui lòng chờ...</div></div>';
    document.body.appendChild(el);
    return el;
  }
  let hideTimer=null;
  function showFloatingStatus(type,title,msg,persist){
    const el=ensureFloatingStatus();
    el.className='show '+(type||'');
    const ico=el.querySelector('.v18-ico');
    const t=el.querySelector('.v18-title');
    const m=el.querySelector('.v18-msg');
    if(ico) ico.textContent=type==='success'?'✓':type==='error'?'!':type==='saving'?'…':'i';
    if(t) t.textContent=title||'Thông báo';
    if(m) m.textContent=msg||'';
    clearTimeout(hideTimer);
    if(!persist) hideTimer=setTimeout(()=>{el.className='';}, type==='error'?5200:3200);
  }
  window.v18ShowSaveStatus=showFloatingStatus;

  function normalizeSwitch(id,onText,offText){
    const sel=document.getElementById(id);
    if(!sel) return;
    const field=sel.closest('.form-field');
    const sw=field && field.querySelector('.smart-switch');
    if(!sw) return;
    sw.dataset.switch=id;
    sw.classList.toggle('force-on',id==='version-force' && String(sel.value)==='1');
    const on=sw.querySelector('.smart-option.on .smart-label');
    const off=sw.querySelector('.smart-option.off .smart-label');
    if(on) on.textContent=onText;
    if(off) off.textContent=offText;
  }
  function normalizeAllSwitches(){
    normalizeSwitch('notice-enabled','BẬT NOTICE','TẮT NOTICE');
    normalizeSwitch('version-enabled','BẬT UPDATE','TẮT UPDATE');
    normalizeSwitch('version-force','BẮT BUỘC UPDATE','KHÔNG BUỘC UPDATE');
  }

  function patchToast(){
    if(window.__v18ToastPatched || typeof window.toast!=='function') return;
    window.__v18ToastPatched=true;
    const old=window.toast;
    window.toast=function(msg,type){
      const isSave=/lưu|save|notice|version|bảo trì|token|trạng thái|thành công|thất bại|lỗi/i.test(String(msg||''));
      if(isSave){
        showFloatingStatus(type==='error'?'error':type==='warn'?'warn':'success', type==='error'?'Thao tác thất bại':'Đã cập nhật', String(msg||''));
      }
      return old.apply(this,arguments);
    };
  }

  function patchApi(){
    if(window.__v18ApiPatched || typeof window.apiForApp!=='function') return;
    window.__v18ApiPatched=true;
    const old=window.apiForApp;
    const labels={setapptoken:'token',resetapptoken:'reset token',setmaintenance:'bảo trì',setversion:'version',setnotice:'notice',setappclosed:'trạng thái app',renameapp:'định danh app'};
    window.apiForApp=async function(appName,params){
      const action=params && params.action;
      const label=labels[action];
      if(label) showFloatingStatus('saving','Đang lưu '+label,'App: '+String(appName||'APP').toUpperCase(),true);
      try{
        const res=await old.apply(this,arguments);
        if(label){
          const ok=!!(res && res.ok && !(res.data && res.data.ok===false));
          showFloatingStatus(ok?'success':'error',ok?'Lưu thành công':'Lưu thất bại',ok?('Đã cập nhật '+label+' cho '+String(appName||'APP').toUpperCase()):(res?(res.raw||('HTTP '+res.status)):'Không nhận được phản hồi'));
        }
        return res;
      }catch(e){
        if(label) showFloatingStatus('error','Lưu thất bại',e && e.message ? e.message : 'Có lỗi khi gửi yêu cầu');
        throw e;
      }
    };
  }

  function patchSwitchRefresh(){
    if(window.__v18SwitchRefreshPatched) return;
    window.__v18SwitchRefreshPatched=true;
    document.addEventListener('change',function(e){
      if(e.target && ['notice-enabled','version-enabled','version-force'].includes(e.target.id)) setTimeout(normalizeAllSwitches,0);
    },true);
  }

  function init(){
    patchToast();
    patchApi();
    patchSwitchRefresh();
    normalizeAllSwitches();
  }
  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',()=>setTimeout(init,140));
  window.addEventListener('load',()=>setTimeout(init,360));
  setInterval(normalizeAllSwitches,1200);
})();
</script>


<script id="v14-dedupe-toast-js">
(function(){
  function removeInlineStatus(){
    var el = document.getElementById('appctl-save-status');
    if(el && el.parentNode) el.parentNode.removeChild(el);
  }

  // Inline App Control status becomes silent.
  function silentStatus(){ removeInlineStatus(); }
  window.v12Status = silentStatus;
  window.setAppctlStatus = silentStatus;
  window.clazyAppctlStatus = silentStatus;
  window.clazySetSaveStatus = silentStatus;

  // Hard de-duplicate every toast path.
  // If old code calls toast twice for the same save action, only the first one is shown.
  function installToastDedupe(){
    var names = ['toast','showToast','notify','showNotify','appToast'];
    names.forEach(function(name){
      var fn = window[name];
      if(typeof fn !== 'function' || fn.__dedupedToast) return;

      var wrapped = function(){
        var args = Array.prototype.slice.call(arguments);
        var msg = args.map(function(v){
          if(v == null) return '';
          if(typeof v === 'object'){
            try{return JSON.stringify(v)}catch(e){return String(v)}
          }
          return String(v);
        }).join('|');

        var now = Date.now();
        var key = name + '|' + msg;

        window.__lastPanelToast = window.__lastPanelToast || {key:'', time:0};
        if(window.__lastPanelToast.key === key && now - window.__lastPanelToast.time < 1800){
          return;
        }

        // Also suppress generic duplicate success after a more specific success.
        var genericSuccess = /thành công|success|đã lưu|lưu thành công|đã gửi/i.test(msg);
        if(genericSuccess && window.__lastPanelToast.time && now - window.__lastPanelToast.time < 650){
          return;
        }

        window.__lastPanelToast = {key:key, time:now};
        return fn.apply(this, arguments);
      };
      wrapped.__dedupedToast = true;
      window[name] = wrapped;
    });
  }

  function patchToastElement(){
    var el = document.getElementById('toast');
    if(!el || el.__dedupePatched) return;
    el.__dedupePatched = true;

    var lastText = '';
    var lastAt = 0;
    var mo = new MutationObserver(function(){
      var txt = (el.textContent || '').trim();
      var now = Date.now();
      if(txt && txt === lastText && now - lastAt < 1800){
        el.classList.remove('show');
        return;
      }
      if(txt){
        lastText = txt;
        lastAt = now;
      }
    });
    mo.observe(el, {childList:true, subtree:true, characterData:true, attributes:true, attributeFilter:['class']});
  }

  function init(){
    removeInlineStatus();
    installToastDedupe();
    patchToastElement();
  }

  if(document.readyState === 'loading') document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', init);
  else init();

  var obs = new MutationObserver(function(){
    removeInlineStatus();
    installToastDedupe();
    patchToastElement();
  });
  obs.observe(document.documentElement, {childList:true, subtree:true});
})();
</script>


<script id="v15-delete-app-patch">
(function(){
  const css = `
  #delete-app-server{
    border-color:rgba(251,113,133,.38)!important;
    background:rgba(251,113,133,.09)!important;
    color:#fb7185!important;
  }
  #delete-app-server:hover{
    background:rgba(251,113,133,.16)!important;
    box-shadow:0 14px 34px rgba(251,113,133,.12)!important;
  }
  .delete-app-hint{
    margin-top:9px;
    font-family:var(--mono);
    font-size:9px;
    line-height:1.55;
    color:rgba(251,113,133,.82);
    border-left:2px solid rgba(251,113,133,.48);
    padding:8px 10px;
    border-radius:12px;
    background:rgba(251,113,133,.055);
  }
  #appctl-grid .app-actions{
    grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr!important;
  }
  #appctl-grid .app-actions .delete-mini{
    border-color:rgba(251,113,133,.32)!important;
    background:rgba(251,113,133,.08)!important;
    color:#fb7185!important;
  }
  @media(max-width:760px){#appctl-grid .app-actions{grid-template-columns:1fr!important}}
  `;
  document.getElementById('delete-app-patch-style')?.remove();
  const st=document.createElement('style');
  st.id='delete-app-patch-style';
  st.textContent=css;
  document.head.appendChild(st);

  function S(v){return String(v ?? '');}
  function E(v){return S(v).replace(/[&<>"']/g,m=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[m]));}
  function J(v){return S(v).replace(/\\/g,'\\\\').replace(/'/g,"\\'").replace(/\n/g,' ');}
  function appLabel(id){
    try{
      const data=(typeof allAppSettings!=='undefined'&&allAppSettings)||window.allAppSettings||{};
      const st=data[id]||{};
      return (st.display_name||st.name||id||'').toString();
    }catch(e){return S(id);}
  }

  async function doDeleteApp(appId){
    if(!appId) return;
    const res = await apiForApp(appId,{action:'deleteapp',admin:'mkadmin',target:appId});
    if(res && res.ok && res.data && res.data.ok){
      const deleted = res.data.deleted || appId;
      if(Array.isArray(res.data.apps)) window.apps = apps = res.data.apps;
      try{
        const m=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('czAppDisplayNames')||'{}');
        delete m[deleted];
        localStorage.setItem('czAppDisplayNames',JSON.stringify(m));
      }catch(e){}
      if(window.currentApp === deleted) window.currentApp = (window.apps && window.apps[0]) || '';
      if(window.editApp === deleted) window.editApp = '';
      toast('Đã xoá app '+S(deleted).toUpperCase(),'success');
      if(typeof renderAppSwitcher==='function') renderAppSwitcher();
      await loadAllAppSettings();
      if(window.currentApp && typeof switchApp==='function') switchApp(window.currentApp);
    }else{
      toast('Xoá app thất bại: '+(res && (res.raw || (res.data && res.data.error)) || 'server error'),'error');
    }
  }

  window.deleteAppFromPanel = function(appId){
    appId = appId || window.editApp;
    if(!appId) return;
    const count = Array.isArray(window.apps || apps) ? (window.apps || apps).length : Object.keys(window.allAppSettings||{}).length;
    if(count <= 1){
      toast('Không thể xoá app cuối cùng','warn');
      return;
    }
    const label = appLabel(appId);
    const msg = 'Xoá app: '+label+'\\nApp ID: '+appId+'\\n\\nServer sẽ xoá khỏi registry và xoá folder/json riêng của app này. Thao tác này không hoàn tác.';
    if(typeof confirm2 === 'function') confirm2('Xoá app này?', msg, ()=>doDeleteApp(appId));
    else if(confirm(msg)) doDeleteApp(appId);
  };

  function ensureDeleteButton(){
    const actions=document.querySelector('#appctl-sec-basic .form-actions');
    if(!actions || document.getElementById('delete-app-server')) return;
    const btn=document.createElement('button');
    btn.type='button';
    btn.id='delete-app-server';
    btn.className='btn action-btn danger';
    btn.textContent='Xoá app';
    btn.onclick=()=>window.deleteAppFromPanel(window.editApp);
    actions.appendChild(btn);

    const sec=document.querySelector('#appctl-sec-basic .form-grid');
    if(sec && !document.getElementById('delete-app-hint')){
      const hint=document.createElement('div');
      hint.id='delete-app-hint';
      hint.className='form-field full delete-app-hint';
      hint.textContent='Xoá app sẽ xoá khỏi danh sách app và xoá dữ liệu JSON riêng của app trên server.';
      sec.appendChild(hint);
    }
  }

  function enhanceCards(){
    const grid=document.getElementById('appctl-grid');
    if(!grid) return;
    grid.querySelectorAll('.app-card').forEach(card=>{
      if(card.querySelector('.delete-mini')) return;
      const appId=card.dataset.app;
      const actions=card.querySelector('.app-actions');
      if(!actions || !appId) return;
      const btn=document.createElement('button');
      btn.type='button';
      btn.className='btn delete-mini';
      btn.textContent='Xoá app';
      btn.onclick=function(ev){ev.stopPropagation();window.deleteAppFromPanel(appId);};
      actions.appendChild(btn);
    });
  }

  const prevEdit=window.editAppSettings;
  if(typeof prevEdit==='function' && !prevEdit.__deletePatch){
    const wrapped=function(){
      const r=prevEdit.apply(this,arguments);
      setTimeout(()=>{ensureDeleteButton();enhanceCards();},0);
      return r;
    };
    wrapped.__deletePatch=true;
    window.editAppSettings=wrapped;
  }

  const prevRender=window.renderAppControl;
  if(typeof prevRender==='function' && !prevRender.__deletePatch){
    const wrapped=function(){
      const r=prevRender.apply(this,arguments);
      setTimeout(enhanceCards,0);
      return r;
    };
    wrapped.__deletePatch=true;
    window.renderAppControl=wrapped;
  }

  const prevLoad=window.loadAllAppSettings;
  if(typeof prevLoad==='function' && !prevLoad.__deletePatch){
    const wrapped=async function(){
      const r=await prevLoad.apply(this,arguments);
      ensureDeleteButton();
      enhanceCards();
      return r;
    };
    wrapped.__deletePatch=true;
    window.loadAllAppSettings=wrapped;
  }

  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',()=>setTimeout(()=>{ensureDeleteButton();enhanceCards();},300));
})();
</script>
