<script id="v19-appctl-slim-motion-js">
(function(){
  function removeEditorCloseOpenButtons(){
    var editor=document.getElementById('appctl-editor');
    if(!editor) return;
    editor.querySelectorAll('button').forEach(function(btn){
      var on=(btn.getAttribute('onclick')||'');
      if(on.indexOf('setAppClosedForEdit')!==-1){
        btn.remove();
      }
    });
    var grid=document.querySelector('#appctl-sec-basic .basic-action-grid');
    if(grid){
      grid.querySelectorAll('.state-close,.state-open').forEach(function(btn){btn.remove();});
    }
  }
  function markSingleCardToggle(){
    document.querySelectorAll('.app-card .app-actions .btn').forEach(function(btn){
      if(/Đóng app|Mở app/.test((btn.textContent||''))){
        btn.setAttribute('title','Đóng/mở app tại card này');
      }
    });
  }
  function afterRender(){
    removeEditorCloseOpenButtons();
    markSingleCardToggle();
  }
  ['editAppSettings','renderAppControl','loadAllAppSettings'].forEach(function(name){
    var fn=window[name];
    if(typeof fn!=='function' || fn.__v19SlimPatched) return;
    var wrapped=function(){
      var res=fn.apply(this,arguments);
      Promise.resolve(res).finally(function(){setTimeout(afterRender,0);});
      return res;
    };
    wrapped.__v19SlimPatched=true;
    window[name]=wrapped;
  });
  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',function(){setTimeout(afterRender,400);});
})();
</script>

<script id="auth-login-30d-lockout">
(function(){
  const PASS_SHA256_HEX = '076795a3f45377ec64a7221976ebbf9aae66d8d5feee12d7cef0687ad9759f38'; //crackditmemay
  const SESSION_KEY = 'cz_auth_session_v1';
  const FAIL_KEY = 'cz_auth_fail_v1';
  const SESSION_TTL_MS = 30 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000; // 30 days
  const LOCK_MS = 30 * 60 * 1000; // 30 minutes
  const MAX_FAIL = 5;

  function now(){ return Date.now(); }
  function readJson(key){
    try{ return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(key) || 'null'); }catch(_){ return null; }
  }
  function writeJson(key, val){
    try{ localStorage.setItem(key, JSON.stringify(val)); }catch(_){}
  }
  function clearKey(key){
    try{ localStorage.removeItem(key); }catch(_){}
  }

  async function sha256Hex(str){
    const enc = new TextEncoder();
    const buf = await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256', enc.encode(String(str || '')));
    return Array.from(new Uint8Array(buf)).map(b=>b.toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('');
  }

  function lockState(){
    const st = readJson(FAIL_KEY) || {count:0, lockUntil:0};
    const until = Number(st.lockUntil || 0);
    if(until && until > now()) return {locked:true, until, count:Number(st.count||0)};
    return {locked:false, until:0, count:Number(st.count||0)};
  }

  function setLocked(until){
    writeJson(FAIL_KEY, {count:0, lockUntil:until});
  }

  function bumpFail(){
    const st = readJson(FAIL_KEY) || {count:0, lockUntil:0};
    const c = Number(st.count || 0) + 1;
    if(c >= MAX_FAIL){
      setLocked(now() + LOCK_MS);
      return {locked:true, remaining:0, triesLeft:0};
    }
    writeJson(FAIL_KEY, {count:c, lockUntil:0});
    return {locked:false, triesLeft:MAX_FAIL - c};
  }

  function clearFail(){
    writeJson(FAIL_KEY, {count:0, lockUntil:0});
  }

  function sessionValid(){
    const s = readJson(SESSION_KEY);
    const exp = s && Number(s.exp || 0);
    return !!exp && exp > now();
  }

  function createSession(){
    writeJson(SESSION_KEY, {exp: now() + SESSION_TTL_MS});
  }

  function clearSession(){
    clearKey(SESSION_KEY);
  }

  function setLockedUI(locked){
    document.body.classList.toggle('auth-locked', !!locked);
    const ov = document.getElementById('auth-ov');
    if(ov) ov.setAttribute('aria-hidden', locked ? 'false' : 'true');
  }

  function fmtRemaining(ms){
    const s = Math.max(0, Math.ceil(ms/1000));
    const m = Math.floor(s/60);
    const r = s % 60;
    return m ? `${m}m ${String(r).padStart(2,'0')}s` : `${r}s`;
  }

  function setMsg(text, type){
    const el = document.getElementById('auth-msg');
    if(!el) return;
    el.className = 'auth-msg' + (type ? ' ' + type : '');
    el.textContent = text || '';
  }

  function setBusy(busy){
    const btn = document.getElementById('auth-btn');
    const inp = document.getElementById('auth-pass');
    if(btn) btn.disabled = !!busy;
    if(inp) inp.disabled = !!busy;
  }

  async function checkAuth(){
    const ls = lockState();
    if(ls.locked){
      setLockedUI(true);
      setMsg('Bị chặn do nhập sai quá 5 lần. Thử lại sau ' + fmtRemaining(ls.until - now()) + '.', 'err');
      return false;
    }
    if(sessionValid()){
      setLockedUI(false);
      return true;
    }
    setLockedUI(true);
    setMsg('Nhập password để tiếp tục.', '');
    return false;
  }

  async function onSubmit(e){
    e.preventDefault();
    const ls = lockState();
    if(ls.locked){
      setMsg('Đang bị chặn. Thử lại sau ' + fmtRemaining(ls.until - now()) + '.', 'err');
      return;
    }

    const passEl = document.getElementById('auth-pass');
    const pass = (passEl && passEl.value) ? passEl.value : '';
    if(!pass){
      setMsg('Vui lòng nhập password.', 'err');
      return;
    }

    setBusy(true);
    try{
      const h = await sha256Hex(pass);
      if(h === PASS_SHA256_HEX){
        createSession();
        clearFail();
        setMsg('OK. Đang mở dashboard...', 'ok');
        setLockedUI(false);
        try{ passEl.value=''; }catch(_){}
        if(typeof window.toast === 'function') try{ window.toast('Đăng nhập thành công','success'); }catch(_){}
        return;
      }
      const r = bumpFail();
      if(r.locked){
        setMsg('Sai password. Bạn đã bị chặn 30 phút.', 'err');
      }else{
        setMsg('Sai password. Còn ' + r.triesLeft + ' lần thử.', 'err');
      }
      if(typeof window.toast === 'function') try{ window.toast('Sai mật khẩu','error'); }catch(_){}
    }finally{
      setBusy(false);
      const l2 = lockState();
      if(l2.locked) setLockedUI(true);
    }
  }

  function install(){
    const form = document.getElementById('auth-form');
    if(form && !form.__authInstalled){
      form.__authInstalled = true;
      form.addEventListener('submit', onSubmit);
    }
    const inp = document.getElementById('auth-pass');
    if(inp && !inp.__authKey){
      inp.__authKey = true;
      inp.addEventListener('keydown', (e)=>{
        if(e.key === 'Escape'){ inp.value=''; setMsg('', ''); }
      });
    }
  }

  // Public helpers
  window.checkAuth = checkAuth;
  window.logout = function(){
    clearSession();
    setLockedUI(true);
    setMsg('Đã đăng xuất.', '');
    if(typeof window.toast === 'function') try{ window.toast('Đã đăng xuất','info'); }catch(_){}
  };

  // Boot
  if(document.readyState === 'loading'){
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', ()=>{ install(); checkAuth(); setTimeout(()=>document.getElementById('auth-pass')?.focus(), 60); });
  }else{
    install(); checkAuth(); setTimeout(()=>document.getElementById('auth-pass')?.focus(), 60);
  }
})();
</script>

@keyframes ios26ToastIn{
  from{opacity:0;transform:translate(-50%,16px) scale(.985);filter:blur(10px)}
  to{opacity:1;transform:translate(-50%,0) scale(1);filter:blur(0)}
}

/* Tick / cross animation */
#toast.show.success::before{
  content:'✓'!important;
  animation:ios26TickIn .42s var(--ios-ease-pop) both!important;
}
#toast.show.error::before{
  content:'✕'!important;
  animation:ios26CrossIn .42s var(--ios-ease-pop) both!important;
}
#toast.show.info::before{
  content:'i'!important;
  animation:ios26InfoIn .38s var(--ios-ease-pop) both!important;
}
#toast.show.warn::before{
  content:'!'!important;
  animation:ios26InfoIn .38s var(--ios-ease-pop) both!important;
}

@keyframes ios26TickIn{
  0%{opacity:0;transform:translateY(6px) scale(.5) rotate(-18deg)}
  55%{opacity:1;transform:translateY(0) scale(1.16) rotate(0deg)}
  100%{opacity:1;transform:translateY(0) scale(1) rotate(0deg)}
}
@keyframes ios26CrossIn{
  0%{opacity:0;transform:translateY(6px) scale(.5) rotate(18deg)}
  55%{opacity:1;transform:translateY(0) scale(1.18) rotate(0deg)}
  100%{opacity:1;transform:translateY(0) scale(1) rotate(0deg)}
}
@keyframes ios26InfoIn{
  0%{opacity:0;transform:translateY(8px) scale(.6)}
  60%{opacity:1;transform:translateY(0) scale(1.14)}
  100%{opacity:1;transform:translateY(0) scale(1)}
}

/* Floating save toast (v18) */
#v18-save-toast{
  background:linear-gradient(180deg,rgba(255,255,255,.085),rgba(255,255,255,.045))!important;
  border:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.18)!important;
  box-shadow:0 26px 90px rgba(0,0,0,.36),0 0 0 1px rgba(255,255,255,.05)!important;
  backdrop-filter:blur(18px) saturate(1)!important;
  -webkit-backdrop-filter:blur(18px) saturate(1)!important;
}

  animation:ios26ToastIn .30s var(--ios-ease-soft) both!important;
}
#v18-save-toast .v18-ico{
  background:rgba(255,255,255,.06)!important;

  color:rgba(255,255,255,.96)!important;

}

  animation:ios26CrossIn .42s var(--ios-ease-pop) both!important;
}
